## Document 050: 20261117 焦點專題：從管晶片到管算力

Pages: 20

### Page 1

誠信 ‧ 承諾 ‧ 創新 ‧ 合作
誠信 ‧ 承諾 ‧ 創新 ‧ 合作
• 報告單位：台新投顧/國際市場研究部
• 報告日期：2026年11月17日
BIS新規將遠端算力納入監管
本報告僅供內部使用
從管晶片到管算力

### Page 2

2
【結論摘要】
四大新規總結
資料來源：U.S. Department of Commerce, BIS, RIN 0694-AK50 IFR（預公告版）；台新投顧整理
① 資料中心算力管制｜新 §744.24
資料中心若對總部位於 D:5 或澳門的企業提供逾 1,200 萬 TPP 算力（約
760 顆 H100），出口相關晶片須申請許可；商業推薦系統經申報後可豁免。
② 東南亞四國＋巴西目的地許可｜§742.6
出口、再出口或境內移轉先進 AI 晶片至印尼、馬來西亞、新加坡、泰國及
巴西，均須申請許可；客戶總部在美國者免許可，但須按月申報。
③ 代工／封測推定收緊｜3A090.a Note 1
設計公司位於或母公司在 D:1、D:4、D:5 或五國者，代工／封測廠不能再
靠電晶體數量推翻「屬 3A090.a」推定。
④ 正式廢除 AI Diffusion Rule
取消國家分層、全球 RS 許可、AIA／LPP 例外、Data Center VEU 與模型
權重管制；保留 ACM 例外與 FDP 規則。

重點：管制範圍從晶片銷售擴大到算力租用。出口時若「知情」，海外資料中心把算力租給中資，出口商也要負責。東南亞轉運熱點直接列入許
可範圍，美系雲端業者相對有利。
說明｜D:5：美國武器禁運國家組（含中國、俄羅斯）；TPP（Total Processing Performance）：衡量晶片運算效能的指標，數值越高效能越強；RS：區域
穩定管制理由；VEU（Validated End-User）：經 BIS 事先核准、可免逐筆申請許可的最終用戶；3A090.a：最高等級的先進運算晶片（如 H100、B200）；
OSAT：委外封裝測試廠；AIA／LPP／ACM：免申請許可的例外通道。

### Page 3

3
【新舊架構對照】
管制架構從「全球配額」轉為「目的地＋用途」審查
資料來源：BIS, RIN 0694-AK50 IFR（預公告版）前言第 I、IV 節；台新投顧整理
面向 AI Diffusion Rule（2025/1，未執行） 本次 IFR
國家架構 三級國家分層＋全球算力配額 取消分層；D:1／D:4／D:5、UAE 及五國須許可，其餘國家無國別許可
遠端算力 未直接規範，僅以政策聲明警示 新 §744.24：對 D:5 企業提供逾 1,200 萬 TPP 算力即須許可
資料中心授權 Universal／National VEU 取消 Data Center VEU，回歸一般 VEU 授權
模型權重 ECCN 4E091＋模型權重 FDP 規則 全數移除
許可例外 AIA、LPP、ACM 刪除 AIA、LPP；保留 ACM（支援非 D:5 終端供應鏈）
監控方式 配額＋VEU 安全標準 個案許可＋美系豁免月報＋KYC 查核（不配合即推定知情）＋用途變更
通知
說明｜三級分層：舊制將各國分為盟友（不設限）、多數國家（設配額）與受管制國家（禁止）三級；FDP：外國用美國技術生產的產品也受美國管轄；

VEU：經 BIS 事先核准、可免逐筆申請許可的最終用戶；許可例外（AIA／LPP／ACM）：符合條件即可免申請許可的通道；D:1／D:4：國安、飛彈技術顧
慮國家組。

### Page 4

4
【新規一｜§744.24】
資料中心遠端算力管制：四個條件同時成立即須許可
資料來源：BIS, RIN 0694-AK50 IFR §744.24(a)–(d)；台新投顧整理
出口、再出口或境內移轉時，若「知情」（knowledge）有下列情形，須取得 BIS 許可；採個案審查，且不適用任何許可例外
A｜產品
3A090（不含 .c HBM）、4A090
及 .z 項目，含 AI 伺服器、相關
軟體與技術
B｜地點
位於全球任何地點的資料中心，
包括以實體或數位方式串連運作
的 linked data centers
C｜規模
提供逾 12,000,000 aggregate
TPP 的算力存取，單一或串連資
料中心合併計算
D｜對象
總部或最終母公司位於 D:5 或澳
門的企業，以及代其訓練或提供
模型的非 D:5 中介業者
豁免：商業推薦系統
僅限排序、篩選及推薦商品或內容的系統（如電商、社群平台），具生成
文字、影像、影音或程式碼能力者不適用。須向 BIS 申報企業名稱、TPP

總量及盡職調查，資料轉交最終用戶審查委員會（End User Review
Committee, ERC）。
既有業者：90 天過渡
規則公布前已對 D:5 企業提供大量算力的資料中心，如欲繼續服務，須於
生效後 90 天內取得許可，否則後續出口將受限制。
說明｜知情（knowledge）：知道或有理由知道；再出口（reexport）：貨品由第三國再出口；境內移轉（transfer in-country）：在同一國內轉手或變更
用途；aggregate TPP：所有晶片算力加總；linked data centers：網路串連、一體運作的多座資料中心；ERC：負責實體清單增刪的跨部會委員會。

### Page 5

5
【算力門檻】
1,200 萬 aggregate TPP 有多大？約 4 櫃GB200 NVL72
資料來源：BIS IFR §744.24、§772.1、3A090；晶片換算依 NVIDIA 公開密集 FP8 規格，台新投顧推估
TPP 估算
TPP ＝ 2 × MacTOPS × 運算位元長度
取原廠宣稱的最高值；稀疏與密集並存時，以密集（dense）計算。
Aggregate TPP：一組 IC 或設備 TPP 的加總（本規則新增於 §772.1）。
例：H100 FP8 密集 1,979 TFLOPS × 8 位元 ≈ TPP 15,832
計算範圍：單一資料中心，或以網路串連、共享資源的 linked data
centers 合計。
≈760
顆 H100／H200
（單顆 TPP ≈ 15,832）
≈330
顆 B200
（單顆 TPP ≈ 36,000）
≈4 櫃
GB200 NVL72
（每櫃 72 顆，TPP ≈ 288 萬）
解讀：門檻不高。有規模的模型訓練或生成式 AI 推論租用，幾乎都會超過；零星的雲端使用不受影響。

說明｜MacTOPS：每秒兆次「乘加」運算數；位元長度：數字精度，FP8＝8 位元；密集運算：不計稀疏化加速的實際算力；TFLOPS／PFLOPS：每秒兆次
／千兆次浮點運算；NVL72：NVIDIA 單櫃搭載 72 顆 GPU 的機櫃系統。

### Page 6

6
【產品算力能力分級】
哪些產品受本次新規管制
資料來源：BIS IFR §742.6(b)(10)(iii)(A)(2)、ECCN 3A090；H200／B200 數值依 NVIDIA 公開規格，台新投顧推估
分類 技術門檻 代表產品 本規則下的管制
3A090.a TPP ≥ 4,800；或 TPP ≥ 1,600 且性能密度 ≥ 5.92 H100／H200、B200／GB200
等資料中心 GPU、ASIC
五國、D 組及 UAE 須許可；適用 §744.24
3A090.b TPP 2,400–4,800 且密度 1.6–5.92；或 TPP ≥ 1,600 且
密度 3.2–5.92
中階 AI 加速器 同 3A090.a
3A090.c HBM，記憶體頻寬密度 > 2 GB/s/mm² HBM 堆疊 僅 D:5／澳門須許可；新規不適用
4A090／.z 內含 3A090 晶片的電腦、伺服器、模組與通訊設備 AI 伺服器、GPU 板卡 比照 3A090；FDP 管轄延伸至整機

對中國的個案審查範圍（延續既有政策）：出口至中國或澳門的產品，若 TPP < 21,000 且 DRAM 總頻寬 < 6,500 GB/s，採個案審查（H200
符合，B200 超出）；其餘 D:5 交易採推定拒絕。
說明｜ECCN：出口管制分類編號；3A090：先進運算晶片，4A090：內含這類晶片的電腦，.z：內含這類晶片的其他設備；性能密度（performance
density）：每平方毫米晶粒的運算效能，避免以加大晶片面積規避門檻；HBM：高頻寬記憶體；FDP：外國製造但用美國技術生產的產品也受美國管轄；
推定拒絕（presumption of denial）：原則上不核准。

7
【新規一｜執行機制】
合規責任延伸至資料中心與後續每一手持有者
資料來源：BIS IFR §744.24 Notes 1–3、(a)(2)、(e)；前言第 III.B 節；台新投顧整理
KYC 盡職調查（Note 3）
出口商須向資料中心取得 KYC 資訊，確認客戶
是否由 D:5 或澳門企業直接或間接持有、控制；
資料中心拒絕或無法提供，即構成警訊（red
flag），推定出口商知情。
持有人責任（Note 1）
禁令適用於所有目前持有晶片者，不限於原始
出口交易的當事人；二手轉售及轉租同樣受規
範。
不適用微量原則（Note 2）
美國原產晶片併入外國產品後，只要供應鏈任
一環節知情將用於禁止用途，即不得適用微量
原則（de minimis）。
用途變更通知｜§744.24(e)
免許可出貨時，出口方須書面通知收貨方：日
後轉供 D:5 企業且超過門檻，須另取得授權；
用途由允許轉為禁止，視同境內移轉。
個別通知機制（is informed）｜(a)(2)
BIS 可透過個別通知或修訂法規，指定特定對

象須申請許可；國務卿或國安顧問通報高風險
地點後，商務部可於 10 天內決定是否施加許
可要求。
既有規定釐清｜§744.23
BIS 重申，D:5 企業為直接或間接採購方，或為資
料中心專屬／主要（逾 50% 算力）遠端使用者時，
即須申請許可；違規者應依 §764.5 自願揭露。
說明｜警訊（red flag）：顯示交易可能有問題的可疑跡象，出口方須先釐清才能出貨，否則視為知情；微量原則（de minimis）：外國產品中美國成分低
於一定比例即不受 EAR 管轄；自願揭露（voluntary self-disclosure）：主動向 BIS 申報違規，可望減輕處罰。

### Page 8

8
【新規二｜§742.6(a)(6)(iii)(A)(1)】
五國列為高轉運風險目的地，美系客戶按月申報即可免許可
資料來源：BIS IFR §742.6、§743.10、摘要與前言第 II 節；台新投顧整理
印尼
馬來西亞
新加坡
泰國
巴西
規則摘要另稱：商務部長可會同
國務卿或國安顧問增列國家
• 管制範圍：3A090（不含 .c）、4A090、.z 項目及相關軟體、技術，出口、再出口與「境內移轉」均須許可。
• 審查政策：個案審查，評估該國出口管制制度有效性，以及與 BIS 執法合作的程度。
• 美系豁免：最終收貨人或使用者的總部或最終母公司在美國 → 免許可，但出口方須每月向 BIS 申報交易明細（§743.10，
本規則新增），內容包括各方名稱與最終母公司、最終目的地、ECCN、品名／SKU、TPP、數量與出貨日，次月首個營業
日前提交；未申報即不適用豁免。
• 國家層級措施：對未有效防堵規避的國家，考慮進口限制、新增出口許可、下調出口管制待遇，以及將相關人士列入
Entity List 或發布暫時拒絕出口令（TDO）。

說明｜轉運（diversion）：晶片先出口至第三國，再轉手至受管制國家；最終收貨人／最終用戶（ultimate consignee／end user）：實際收貨與使用晶片
的公司；實體清單（Entity List）：列入者出口須許可，且多半不獲核准；暫時拒絕出口令（TDO）：可立即停止特定對象的出口權利。§743.10：本規則新
增的申報條文，美系客戶免許可的前提是出口方按月申報；RS（Regional Stability）：區域穩定管制理由，本規則改為僅適用於五國。

准（presumption of denial／approval）：原則上不核准／原則上核准；個案審查（case-by-case）：無預設立場、逐案判斷；supp. no. 8 to part 740：
EAR 附錄中 UAE 的核准實體名單。

【新規三｜3A090.a Note 1、§744.23(a)(3)(i)(C)】
晶圓代工／封測（OSAT）推定收緊
資料來源：BIS IFR 前言第 III、V 節、ECCN 3A090 Note 1、§744.23(a)(3)(i)(C)；台新投顧整理
代工或封測廠出貨 16/14nm 以下或採非平面電晶體的邏輯 IC，推定屬 3A090.a 資料中心晶片；如需排除，須透過下列途徑之一證明
途徑 內容 本次變更
a｜設計公司證明 核准（supp. 6）或授權 IC 設計公司提供 TPP 與性能密度證明 不變；2026 年底前授權設計公司＝總部在台灣或 A:1／
A:5，且與 D:5 無關
b｜代工廠封裝＋電晶
體數
前段代工廠於非 D:5 地點封裝，證明成品電晶體數 < 300 億（無 HBM
：2027 年 < 350 億；2029 年起 < 400 億）
設計公司位於（或母公司在）D:1、D:4、D:5 或五國者，
不得再適用
c｜核准 OSAT＋電晶體
數
由核准 OSAT（supp. 7）證明同上門檻 同上，不得再適用

新增許可：代工或封測廠為非核准、非授權設計公司生產 3A090.a 先進邏輯 IC，出貨至任何目的地均須許可。中國及東南亞背景的設計公司實
際上僅能透過途徑 a 證明。
說明｜前段代工：晶圓製造廠，如台積電；OSAT：委外封裝測試廠，如日月光；推定（presumption）：先認定屬管制晶片，除非能提出反證；非平面電
晶體：FinFET、GAA 等先進製程結構；核准／授權設計公司：BIS 認可、可出具算力證明的設計公司；A:1／A:5：EAR 國家組，A:1＝瓦聖納協定成員，A:5
＝美國盟友（兩者大致重疊，A:5 另含 UAE）；過去以電晶體數量判斷是否為 AI 晶片，但晶片可拆成多顆小晶片再組裝，新規改以設計公司所在地判斷。

【新規四｜廢除 AI Diffusion Rule】
取消配額與國家分級，保留供應鏈例外與 FDP 管轄
資料來源：BIS IFR 前言第 IV 節、§734.9、§740、§748.15；台新投顧整理
移除
• 三級國家分層與全球 Regional Stability 許可要求
• License Exception AIA（§740.27）與 LPP（§740.29）
• Data Center VEU（Universal／National VEU）及 supp. 10 to part
748
• 模型權重管制：ECCN 4E091、Red Flag 28、模型權重 FDP 規則
§734.9(l)
• §772.1「Model Weights」定義
保留／重申
• License Exception ACM（§740.28）：支援終端在 D:5／澳門以外的供
應鏈
• 先進運算 FDP 規則 §734.9(h) 的全球適用範圍
• 3A090 晶片嵌入板卡或伺服器後，BIS 仍保有管轄

• D:5 總部企業許可要求回歸 §744.23(a)(3)（2023/11 起即存在）
意涵：美國閉源模型部署到海外，不再受模型權重管制。中東等五國以外的市場改為個案許可，算力配額上限取消。
說明｜許可例外：符合條件即免逐筆申請許可；AIA／LPP：舊制給盟友與小量出口的例外；ACM：供製造組裝用的例外；模型權重：AI 模型訓練出的參數
，等同模型本體；FDP：外國用美國技術生產的產品也受管。

### Page 12

12
【政府如何監管】
監管方式：事前許可、事中申報、事後追溯與國家層級措施
資料來源：BIS IFR §742.6、§743.10、§744.24、摘要；台新投顧整理
事前｜許可審查
• §744.24 與五國個案許可
• D:5 推定拒絕、D:1／D:4 推定核
准
• BIS 個別通知（is informed），
指定對象或地點須許可
事中｜申報與 KYC
• 美系豁免按月申報（§743.10）
• 推薦系統須申報，資料轉交 ERC
• 資料中心須配合 KYC，拒絕即推
定知情
事後｜追溯
• 用途變更須授權，視同境內移轉
• 任一手持有人皆受禁令拘束
• 紀錄保存、自願揭露與執法
國家層級
• 新增指定高轉運風險國
• 進口限制、下調管制待遇
• Entity List、暫時拒絕出口令
監管架構：BIS 不直接管雲端服務，而是從出口環節下手：以出口時是否知情為基礎，再加上持有人責任與用途變更責任。結果是晶片供應商、
伺服器廠與資料中心必須互相查核，部分執法工作等於交給供應鏈與所在國政府。

說明｜ERC（End User Review Committee）：商務、國務、國防、能源部組成的跨部會委員會，決定實體清單增刪；推定知情：資料中心不配合查核時，
視同出口方知情；境內移轉：同國內轉手或改變用途；下調管制待遇：將該國改列至管制較嚴格的國家組；TDO：暫時拒絕出口令。

### Page 14

【影響評估】
資料來源：台新投顧觀點
管制重心從「晶片賣給誰」
延伸到「算力租給誰」
出口商只要「知情」，海外資料中心把算力租給中資，就可能違反出口管制；
中資經東南亞租用算力的管道將明顯收窄；美系雲端在五國免許可，競爭位置反而變好。

### Page 15

15
【影響（一）｜產業】
中資租戶與東南亞 AIDC 受壓，美系雲端相對受惠
資料來源：BIS IFR；台新投顧整理與推估，影響方向為定性判斷，非個股投資建議
產業／代表公司 影響 主要邏輯
NVIDIA、AMD 中性偏負 東南亞中資租用需求受限，出貨文件變多、交期拉長；美系雲端需求不受影響，對中國出口
H200 的個案審查管道也還在
AI 伺服器 ODM（鴻海、廣達、緯穎、緯
創、英業達）
中性，交期風險 出貨五國須許可或按月申報，合規成本與交期不確定性上升；ACM 例外保留，供應鏈轉運影
響有限
東南亞資料中心營運商／neocloud 負面 中資的訓練與生成式租戶須許可，且恐難獲准；KYC 成本上升，以中資客戶為主的擴建計畫可
能延後
美系雲端（微軟、Google、亞馬遜） 正面 總部在美國，免許可；中資退出五國後留下的需求，最可能由它們承接
中國 AI／雲端（字節跳動、阿里巴巴、
騰訊）
負面 海外租用算力的管道受限；生成式 AI 不適用推薦系統豁免，預期加速轉向國產晶片，並申請

H200 許可
台積電、日月光等代工封測 中性 營收影響有限，但客戶篩選與合規成本上升；中國及東南亞背景的設計客戶只能走途徑 a 證明
說明｜AIDC：AI 資料中心；neocloud：專門出租 GPU 算力的新興雲端業者，如 CoreWeave；ODM：替品牌代工設計與製造的廠商；ACM 例外：為製造
、組裝目的移轉晶片可免許可；途徑 a：由核准／授權設計公司出具算力證明；中性偏負：整體影響不大，但方向偏不利。

【影響（二）｜區域】
東南亞 AIDC 納入許可管制，區域投資須重新評估客戶結構
資料來源：BIS IFR；Export Compliance Daily（2025/8）、The Information 經 Tom’s Hardware 轉述（2026/8）；台新投顧整理
馬來西亞
柔佛 AIDC 受影響最大，中資租戶多的業者面
臨許可與 90 天期限。大馬 2025/7 已自設 AI
晶片出口許可，執法合作將左右審查寬嚴。

過去多起輝達 GPU 轉運執法案件都經過新馬。
中資雲端業者在當地的據點，客戶與資料中心

泰國
媒體報導泰國是中資租用算力的管道之一。泰
國須先證明自身的出口管制能力，才可能爭取
較寬鬆的審查。
印尼
AIDC 投資才剛起步就被納入管制。美系雲端

審批不確定性升高。
巴西
五國中唯一不在亞洲的國家，可見美方已把拉
美 AIDC 視為轉運風險。當地資料中心投資案
若有中資客戶，須重新盤點。
中東（UAE 等）

不在五國名單內。UAE 核准實體免許可，D:1
／D:4 推定核准，處境相對有利，可能承接部
分轉移需求。
說明｜柔佛（Johor）：馬來西亞南部、緊鄰新加坡的資料中心聚落；轉運執法案件：美國起訴經第三國轉運輝達晶片的案件；審查待遇：BIS 審許可時對該
國的寬嚴程度；轉移需求：受限的算力需求改往其他地區；推定核准：申請原則上會被批准。

### Page 17

17
【影響（三）｜算力：計算說明】
新規約影響 2027 全球新增算力 2–3.5%，上限約 7%
資料來源：IFP（2026）、Epoch AI（Chip Sales、Chip Smuggling，2026）；台新投顧整理與假設
STEP 1｜分母：全球算力
2026 年底全球存量：約 4,600 萬
H100e（Epoch AI）
2027 年全球新增：假設 4,000 萬（產
能大幅開出）
2027 年底存量：4,600 ＋ 4,000 ≈
8,600，取整約 9,000 萬
→ 新增 4,000 萬／存量 9,000 萬
STEP 2｜分子：遠端存取
IFP 估計：中資遠端租用算力：今年約
100 萬 → 2027 年底 280 萬
交叉驗證：880 萬（IFP預估中國可取得
總算力）× 65%（尚未取得、 2027 年
才到位）× ½（遠端）≈ 285 萬
2027 年新增：280 萬 − 100 萬（今年
已在使用）＝ 180 萬
→ 受影響增量約 180 萬
STEP 3｜加上走私

走私：Epoch 估計至 2025 年底累計約
66 萬（區間 29–160 萬）
假設：2027 年單年走私 30–100 萬（台
新假設）
計算：低情境下 180 ＋ 30 ＝ 210 萬；
高情境下 180 ＋ 100 ＝ 280 萬
→ 毛額 210～280 萬
STEP 4｜扣掉轉單（淨額）
需求會轉向：推薦系統豁免、個案許可
、H200、國產晶片、非中資租戶
假設：50–60% 轉向其他管道，實際流
失 40–50%
計算：210 × 40% ≈ 85；280 × 50% ＝
140
→ 淨額 85～140 萬
STEP 5｜換算占比＝分子 ÷ 分母
分子（萬 H100e） ÷ 全球新增 4,000 萬 ÷ 全球存量 9,000 萬 ÷ NVIDIA 2,400 萬*
遠端存取新增量 180 4.5% 2.0% 7.5%
含走私量 210–280 5.3–7.0% 2.3–3.1% 8.8–11.7%
扣除轉單（淨額）85–140 2.1–3.5% 0.9–1.6% 3.5–5.8%

* NVIDIA 新增＝4,000 萬 × 假設市占 60%；遠端存取幾乎都是 NVIDIA 晶片
說明｜IFP：美國智庫 Institute for Progress；Epoch AI：追蹤全球 AI 晶片與算力的研究機構；分子：受新規影響的算力，分母：比較基準；65%、½ 為 IFP 對中國算力來源的拆分。
4.5%  遠端存取增量占 2027 全球新增算力
5–7%  加計走私曝險上限
2–3.5%  扣除轉單後的淨影響

名詞定義
資料來源：BIS IFR §744.24(d)、§772.1、ECCN 3A090 Technical Notes；台新投顧整理
名詞 定義（依 IFR 及 EAR）
Country Group D:5 美國武器禁運國家組，含中國、俄羅斯等；本規則另將澳門比照處理
TPP Total Processing Performance＝2 × MacTOPS × 運算位元長度，取最高值、以密集運算計
Aggregate TPP 一組 IC 或設備 TPP 的加總（新增於 §772.1）
Linked data centers 以網路基礎設施串連、可共享資源、平衡負載並複製資料而整體運作的多個資料中心，含數位連結
Commercial recommender system 專為且僅用於依使用者行為、交易紀錄或品項屬性排序、篩選、推薦商品或內容的 AI 工具（原文稱 SI）；任何生成
式系統或內含生成模型者均排除
知情（knowledge） EAR 用語，含知道或有理由知道；資料中心拒絕提供 KYC 資訊即構成警訊（red flag），推定知情

Front-end fabricator／OSAT 前段晶圓製造廠／委外封裝測試廠
Ultimate parent 最終母公司；管制以總部或最終母公司所在地判定，不論子公司註冊地

### Page 19

19
【附錄】
中英名詞對照
資料來源：BIS IFR、EAR（15 CFR Parts 730–774）；台新投顧整理
中文（英文原文） 白話說明
知情（knowledge） 知道或有理由知道交易涉及禁止用途；對明顯疑點刻意不查，也算知情
推定（presumption） 先認定屬某種情況，除非能提出反證
推定拒絕／推定核准（presumption of denial／approval） 許可申請原則上不核准／原則上核准
個案審查（case-by-case review） 無預設立場，逐案判斷是否核准
警訊（red flag） 顯示交易可能有問題的可疑跡象；出口方須先釐清才能出貨
個別通知（is informed） BIS 以書面通知特定業者，指定某些交易須申請許可
再出口（reexport） 貨品由第三國再出口至另一國
境內移轉（transfer in-country） 在同一國內轉手，或變更最終用途
最終收貨人／最終用戶（ultimate consignee／end user） 實際收貨／實際使用貨品的對象

微量原則（de minimis） 外國產品所含美國成分低於一定比例，即不受 EAR 管轄
許可例外（License Exception） 符合特定條件，即可免逐筆申請許可
外國直接產品規則（Foreign Direct Product rule, FDP） 外國以美國技術或設備生產的產品，同樣受 EAR 管轄
在途貨物緩衝條款（savings clause） 規則刊登時已在運送途中的貨物，可依舊規定完成交貨
自願揭露（voluntary self-disclosure） 主動向 BIS 申報違規，可望減輕處罰
暫行最終規則（Interim Final Rule, IFR） 刊登後即生效，事後再徵詢意見並可能修正的法規

## Document 124: 261112_daiwa_eink

Source PDF: all report pdf/261112_daiwa_eink.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-11-12
Pages: 5

### Page 1

Summary: E Ink held an investor call today, 12 November, after 3Q26 results were 
announced on 6 November (see our memo). Overall, its 3Q26 gross margin of 52.2% (vs. 
58.7% in 2Q26 and 56.0% in 3Q25) missed our estimate of 59.0% and the consensus 
estimate of 58.2%, mainly due to less revenue scale and unfavourable product mix within 
consumer electronics segment (ie, more module, less film). E Ink also talked about their 
cooperation with Taiflex (8039 TT, not rated) on PTFE material and management is 
optimistic towards the development progress.

We have a Hold (3) rating on E Ink with a 12-month TP of TWD165, based on a target PER 
of 14x applied to our 1-year forward EPS forecast. For more information on the company, 
please refer to our latest flash, Downgrading: weaker-than-expected order visibility, on 13 
August 2026.

What’s the impact  
• 3Q26 results recap. E Ink’s 3Q26 revenue of TWD9,936m (-2.7% QoQ, -4.6% YoY) 
was roughly in line with our and the Bloomberg consensus estimates. Its 3Q26 gross 
margin of 52.2% (vs. 58.7% in 2Q26 and 56.0% in 3Q25) missed our estimate of 
59.0% and the consensus estimate of 58.2%, mainly due to less revenue scale and 
unfavourable product mix within its consumer electronics segment (ie, more module, 
less film). Its 3Q26 net profit of TWD2,652m (-29.0% QoQ, -37.3% YoY) beat our and 
consensus estimates by 21.6% and 15.2%, respectively. Its 3Q26 EPS came in at 
TWD2.29. Note E Ink’s 3Q26 product mix was: consumer electronics 54.6% (-9.4% 
YoY), IoT (including ESL and large signage) 45.4% (+1.8% YoY). 
• 2026 and 2027 guidance. E Ink slightly revised down its 2026 full-year guidance: total 
revenue to grow by 9% YoY (down from previous 10-15%); consumer electronics to 
decline by 15% YoY, negatively affected by the memory price hikes; IoT to grow by 
35%+ YoY; and signage to account for high-single-digit % of total revenue. For 2027, 
the company is still positive on the growth of the IoT segment, but consumer the 
consumer electronics segment will depend on how the new generations of end 
products perform. 
• Cooperation with Taiflex (8039 TT, not rated) on PTFE. E Ink has announced it will 
work with Taiflex to co-develop the next generation of CCL material – PTFE. The two 
companies will combine Taiflex’s expertise in CCL and high-end electronics materials, 
and E Ink’s capabilities in mass production, surface treatment and roll-to-roll 
processing. E Ink expects to see preliminary results by 2027 and significant revenue 
contribution from 2028. 
• Capacity expansion and future trends. E Ink’s H5 plant is performing on track and 
its utilisation rate and yield rate are improving. The H6 plant has also started to install 
equipment in November 2026, and is scheduled for a trial run in 2027 and contribution 
to revenue in 2028. The company expects both plants to have capability to 
manufacture large signage. Combining the trend that E Ink’s customers and the 
retailers are now planning to implement more larger signages (mid-sized, 10-15 
inches, vs. 2 inches for ESL) as retail advertisement, the company is confident that 
signage will replace ESL to become the fastest growing segment.

Country

capacity expansion being early/delayed.

### Page 1

E Ink Holdings

Cuts 2026 guidance again; PTFE mass 
production in 2028; reiterate Neutral

Reiterate Rating: NEUTRAL | PO: 148.00 TWD | Price: 140.00 TWD

Cuts ’26 sales guidance to +9% YoY; IoT as key ’27 driver  
Mgmt. cut its 2026 guidance again and now expects overall sales to +9% YoY (prior: up 
10-15% YoY; Jan-Oct sales +4% YoY). It expects IoT sales up 35%+ YoY (BofAe: +39% 
YoY, with ESL sales +32% and signage sales more than doubling) and CE sales -15% YoY. 
Mgmt. cited weaker CE demand and still expects signage at single-digit% of 2026 sales 
(BofAe: 7%). The latest full-year guidance implies a mid- to high single-digit% MoM 
decline in Nov and Dec. Mgmt. gave no numeric 2027 guidance, but it still sees ESL and 
signage (especially mid-size) as the key drivers, while CE order visibility stays low. 
PTFE in focus on 3Q26 call; mass production likely in ’28 
E Ink’s PTFE collaboration with Taiflex (8039 TT; not covered) was one of the main 
topics on the 3Q26 call. We now take a neutral view on E Ink’s PTFE business. We see its 
strong R&D capability in material modification and strength in roll-to-roll manufacturing, 
but mgmt. said the business model is still under discussion and mass production could 
occur in 2028. As a result, our estimates currently do not include any PTFE contribution. 
3Q26 recap: less-favorable mix weighs on margins 
OP was 21%/17% below BofAe/consensus as GM/OPM of 52.2%/26.6% came in lower 
than expected (6.1ppt/7.0ppt below BofAe). Mgmt. said the weaker GM came from a 
higher mix of lower-margin module business. NP was 20%/15% below BofAe/consensus. 
Reiterate Neutral; cut PO to NT$148 on lower P/E 
We cut our 2026E EPS by 8% but lift our 2027-28E EPS by 0-5% to reflect 3Q26 miss 
and the latest guidance. We trim our PO to NT$148 (12x 2027E EPS) from NT$185 (15x 
2027E EPS), with a lower multiple on reduced CE demand visibility and macro risk. 12x is 
similar to ESL system integrators’ P/E average. We reiterate our Neutral given moderate 
sales growth, still-expanding margins, and fair valuation (Exhibit 4).

Company Sector 
Electronics

Company Description 
E Ink is top global e-paper supplier with over 90% market 
share. E Ink was originally spun out of MIT Media Lab in 
1997 and was acquired by Prime View International in 
2009. The two companies were combined/branded as E Ink 
into e-paper supply chain. Besides its headquarters in 
Hsinchu (Taiwan), E Ink has facilities in Linkou (Taiwan), 
Yangzhou (China), and South Hadley (US).

Investment Rationale 
We have a Neutral rating on E Ink given moderate 2026 
guidance and 2027 sales growth with potential 
demand/order risk due to component shortage and macro 
uncertainty. Yet we still like E Ink's dominant position in 
global e-paper market with over 90% market share, high 
margins thanks to higher mix from better-margin IoT 
business, and positive OCF/FCF.

OBAL RESEARCH

Price objective basis & risk 
E Ink Holdings (PVWIF) 
We derive our PO of NT$148 from 12x 2027E EPS. Our target P/E multiple is set at the 
average of global key ESL system integrators. It is also at the lower end of 11-34x P/E 
range since AI era from 2023. We believe E Ink's valuation has been re-set with a lower 
premium after AI as its business is less directly exposed to AI.

Upside risks: (1) stronger market demand in consumer electronics, (2) faster ESL 
demand/adoption, (3) faster adoption and more applications in various vertical markets 
like smart transportation/city and smart medical, (4) more favorable USD/RMB 
movement to drive margin expansion and/or non-operating items, and (5) higher non-
operating royalty income due to stronger TFT-LCD demand.

Downside risks: (1) much weaker-than-expected consumer demand, (2) slower-than-
expected ESL demand/adoption to replace from conventional labels, (3) lower utilization 
rate to weigh on margins under higher capex and depreciation costs, (4) higher raw 
material cost and/or logistics cost, (5) lower non-operating royalty income due to weaker 
TFT-LCD demand, and (6) unfavorable FX movement (USD/RMB and/or USD/NTD) to 
weigh on GM and/or non-operating items.

potential as well as its attractiveness for investment relative to other stocks within its Coverage Cluster (defined below). Our investment ratings are: 1 - Buy stocks 
are expected to have a total return of at least 10% and are the most attractive stocks in the coverage cluster; 2 - Neutral stocks are expected to remain flat or 
increase in value and are less attractive than Buy rated stocks and 3 - Underperform stocks are the least attractive stocks in a coverage cluster. An investment

## Document 138: 261113_daiwa_Gigabyte

### Page 1

Summary: Gigabyte released its 3Q26 results after market hours on 13 November, with 
net income of TWD8.57bn (EPS: TWD12.07) beating expectations, while revenue of 
TWD154.7bn (+7% QoQ, +94% YoY) was largely in line despite robust server demand. 
Gross margin expanded 4.4pp QoQ to 14.0% (vs. our 9.6% estimate). Management cited 
factors such as higher value-added products and better cost control, though we believe 
VGA card price hikes were the key driver. Despite strong gross margin expansion and 
operating leverage, higher profit-sharing bonuses limited OPM expansion to 2.7pp QoQ to 
8.3%, still above our/consensus estimates of 5.8%/5.9%. We expect Gigabyte to benefit 
from solid AI server demand from neoclouds as they increase power budgets and capex, 
with neoclouds and enterprises becoming key Nvidia server buyers from Vera Rubin in 
2027. Nebius reaffirmed its 5GW contracted-power and 0.8-1GW connected-power end-
2026 targets, with >1GW annual deployments from 2027; CoreWeave raised its 2026 
capex guidance to USD37-39bn, with USD132.5bn in backlog. As neoclouds serve 
sovereign and enterprise customers lacking in-house hardware capabilities, we expect 
TAM growth for server brands including Dell, Gigabyte, Lenovo and Asustek. We reaffirm 
our AI server revenue forecasts of TWD412bn in 2026E and TWD565bn in 2027E. We 
expect the AI server mix to dilute the GM to 8.9% in 2027E and 8.4% in 2028E. We 
reiterate our Buy (1) rating with a 12M TP of TWD490, based on 15x the 1-year-forward 
diluted EPS; key downside risk is Nvidia’s business model change.

What’s the impact: 
Below are our read-throughs from Gigabyte’s 3Q26 results (released on 13 November).

• 3Q26 results first-take: profitability beat expectations. Gigabyte’s net income of 
TWD8.57bn (EPS: TWD12.07) beat our/Bloomberg consensus estimates by 32%/29%, 
respectively. Revenue (TWD154.7bn; up 7% QoQ and 94% YoY) was largely in-line with 
estimates, despite robust server demand. Nevertheless, the gross margin expanded by 
4.4pp QoQ to 14.0% in 3Q26 (exceeding our estimate of 9.6%). Management attributed 
the strength to an improved product mix, higher value-added products, strategic 
procurement, and better cost control, but we believe price hikes in VGA cards to be the 
key factor. Despite strong gross margin expansion and operating leverage, higher profit-
sharing bonus expenses caused the operating margin to only rise by 2.7pp QoQ to 8.3% 
(still beating our/street’s estimates of 5.8%/5.9%).

• Business outlook. We believe Gigabyte will ride on consistently solid AI server demand 
from the neoclouds, as key vendors continue to raise their power budget and capex. 
This aligns with our view that the neoclouds and enterprise will gradually become key

buyers of Nvidia AI servers starting from the Vera Rubin generation in 2027. Nebius 
(NBIS US, NR) reaffirmed its end-2026 contracted-power target of 5GW (up from >4GW 
currently contracted) and connected-power target of 0.8-1GW, with plans to deploy 
>1GW annually starting in 2027. Meanwhile, Coreweave (CRWV US, NR) raised its 
2026 capex guidance to USD37-39bn (from USD35-39bn) with an order backlog of 
USD132.5bn (vs. USD3.7bn revenue in 3Q26). We believe the neoclouds will become a 
key computing source for sovereign, enterprise and other corporate customers that 
cannot design and maintain the hardware themselves. As such, we expect the TAM for 
server brands like Dell (DELL US, USD371.17, Outperform [2], covered by Louis 
Miscioscia), Gigabyte, Lenovo (992 HK, NR) and Asustek (2357 TT, NR) to rise.

While management expects continued revenue growth and improving operating 
leverage in 2027, we reaffirm our AI server revenue assumptions for Gigabyte of 
TWD412bn in 2026E and TWD565bn in 2027E (vs. TWD180bn in 2025). We also 
believe the rising contribution from AI servers will be structurally margin-dilutive, despite 
potential benefits from higher value-added solutions, strategic procurement, and 
economies of scale. We therefore forecast gross margin to gradually normalise to 8.9% 
in 2027E and 8.4% in 2028E (vs. 14.0% in 3Q26).

What we recommend: 
We have a Buy (1) rating on Gigabyte, with a 12M TP of TWD490, based on a target PER 
of 15x, applied to our 1-year-forward diluted EPS. Key downside risk: earnings impact from 
Nvidia’s business model change.

4971.TT IET-KY 
 400G 磊晶、QD Laser 的先鋒

[法說會更新]   
   公司簡介 
英特磊科技股份有限公司(IET-KY)於 2011 年 4 月設立，為全球磷化銦(InP)、銻化鎵
(GaAs)、銻化鎵(GaSb)磊晶片代工領導廠，提供無線通訊、光纖通訊等產品應用。3Q26
營收組成為 GaAs 36.5%、InP 33.4%、GaSb 和勞務收入 26.5%與其它 3.5%。 
投資評等/目標價 重點摘要 
區間操作 424 
【前次投資建議】無 
【大盤指數/股價】43092 / 400

➢ 3Q26 出貨量回升，4Q26 認列設備營收，2026 年營收年增 17.4% 
由於 InP 基板供貨恢復穩定使 Epi-Wafer 出貨量成長，IET-KY 3Q26 營
收 3.2 億元(YoY+26.8%，QoQ+24.6%)。毛利率 38.7%(YoY+7ppt)提
升係因高毛利水準之磊晶片及硬體銷售所帶動。EPS 1.26 元。3Q26 營
收組成為 GaAs 36.5%、InP 33.4%、GaSb 和勞務收入 26.5%與其它
3.5%。各項產品線營運狀況如下：(1)GaAs：射頻與國防應用合計占其
比重約 20%，營收年增 72%。(2)InP：APD/PD 需求極為強勁，但因基
板供應受限，營收年減 2%。考量客製化修改特殊設備營收預計於 12 月
認列，且基板供貨穩定，研究員預期 12 月營收高於 10~11 月，預計
4Q26 營收季增 12.4%至 3.6 億元(YoY+12.7%)，預估 2026 年營收 12.7
億元(YoY+17.4%)，毛利率 40%，EPS 4.85 元。 
➢ 2027 年 400G EAM 與 Driver 開始量產，產能年增 10%至 20% 
展望 2027 年，GaAs 方面，國防訂單與商用訂單同步增加 30%。VCSEL
在新增機台後產能估約增加 40%，預估 GaAs 營收年增 20%至 25%。
第一位客戶已突破 InP 基板限制後，預計 2027 年開始量產 400G EAM
與 Driver。若 2027 年基板供應能維持現有水準，預 期 InP Epi-Wafer 出
貨量將有雙位數成長。在 GaSb 方面，公司著重於生物辨識等創新應用，
並已建置第二條 GaSb 生產線，預計於 2027 年正式量產。配合晶片法
案承諾，公司將於 2027 年購買 2 至 3 台機台，機台總數增至 18 台至
19 台，總產能約增加 10%至 20%，預估 2027 年營收 16.1 億元
(YoY+27.1%)，毛利率 41.6%，EPS 6.40 元。 
➢ 初次建議區間操作，目標價 424 元 
IET-KY 作為全球唯一一家採用 MBE 製程量產磊晶的供應商，客戶包含
多家全球雷射大廠。在產品規格上，公司已踏入 400G EAM 與 Driver、
200G VCSEL、QD Laser 等高規格產品所用之 Epi-Wafer 的研發，且
400G EAM 與 Driver、QD Laser 於 2027 年就會開始出貨，產品進程遠
遠領先同業。然而，在 400G 產品面臨龐大需求時，成本較低的 MOCVD
勢必取代初期採用的 MBE 成為主要技術。參考光通訊產業 Forward PE
評價多落在 20X~50X 區間，以 2028 年 EPS 換算，目前為 49X，評價
偏高。初次建議區間操作，目標價 424 元(2028F PE 52X)

4971 IET-KY 加權指數

### Page 2

個股報告

公司介紹 
英特磊科技股份有限公司(IET-KY)於 2011 年 4 月設立，為全球磷化銦(InP)、銻化鎵
(GaAs)、銻化鎵(GaSb)磊晶片代工領導廠，是少數採用 MBE 技術從事生產的磊晶供
應商，具有基板到磊晶的垂直整合技術能力，提供無線通訊、衛星通訊、光纖通訊等
商業用途，以及太空與國防上的紅外線感測、夜視、攝影等產品應用。3Q26 營收組
成為 GaAs 36.5%、InP 33.4%、GaSb 和勞務收入 26.5%與其它 3.5%。 
資料來源:國票投顧 
圖 1：IET-KY 營收組成

3Q26 出貨量回升，4Q26 認列設備營收，2026 年營收年增 17.4% 
由於 InP 基板供貨恢復穩定使 Epi-Wafer 出貨量成長，IET-KY 3Q26 營收 3.2 億元
(YoY+26.8%，QoQ+24.6%)。毛利率季增 4.7ppt 至 38.7%(YoY+7ppt)提升係因高
毛利水準之磊晶片及硬體銷售所帶動。EPS 1.26 元。3Q26 營收組成為 GaAs 36.5%、
InP 33.4%、GaSb 和勞務收入 26.5%與其它 3.5%。應用比重為射頻 19.6%、汽車防
撞及車用 13.3%、光纖網路 24.6%、高速傳輸元件 13.7%、紅外線偵測 25.2%、硬體
構件 3.6%。各項產品線營運狀況如下：(1)GaAs：射頻與國防應用合計占其比重約
20%，營收年增 72%。(2)InP：受惠於 5G 基礎建設、高頻光纖網路與雲端運算發展，
APD/PD 需求極為強勁，100G/200G PD 占整體營收 15%至 20%，但因基板供應受
限，營收年減 2%。(3)GaSb：由於國防客戶快速加單，商用訂單持續擴大，3Q26 營
收占比由 15%升至 26.5%，營收年增 18%。考量客製化修改特殊設備營收預計於 12
月認列，且基板供貨穩定，研究員認為 11 月與 10 月營收水準差異不大，12 月營收
高於 11 月，預計 4Q26 營收季增 12.4%至 3.6 億元(YoY+12.7%)，預估 2026 年營
收 12.7 億元(YoY+17.4%)，毛利率 40%，EPS 4.85 元。 
2027 年 m-PIN、400G EAM 與 Driver 開始量產，產能年增 10%至 20% 
展望 2027 年，各產品線發展如下：(1)GaAs：國防訂單與商用訂單同步增加 30%。
m-PIN(變晶型 PIN)可望進入量產階段。VCSEL 在新增機台後產能估約增加 40%。二
次長晶已由淡水廠接單，新機台即將完成驗收，已獲得固定客戶下單並進行認證，2027
年可支應客戶需求，預估 GaAs 營收年增 20%至 25%。(2)InP：第一位客戶已突破 InP

### Page 3

- 3 -

個股報告 
基板限制，預計 2027 年開始量產 400G EAM 與 Driver。在歐洲供應商導入，以及研
磨、晶片表面拋光重複使用等技術逐漸成熟下，若 2027 年基板供應能維持現有水準，
預期 InP Epi-Wafer 出貨量將有雙位數成長。(3)GaSb：公司著重於生物辨識等創新
應用，並已建置第二條 GaSb 生產線，預計於 2027 年正式量產。在公司新增機台後，
GaSb 產能估增加 30%。(4)勞務收入(硬體構件)：客製化新材料機台代營運及「由租
轉買」合約已接近完成。產能方面，在二期廠房擴建工程方面，經過審核與跨政黨行
政團隊過渡，法律與技術問題已全數解決，德州晶片法案補助款亦已陸續領取並支應
擴建工程，預計 4Q26 內完成簽署正式合約，1Q27 完工，可望於 2H27 開始挹注營
收。同時，配合晶片法案承諾，公司將於 2027 年購買 2 至 3 台機台，機台總數增至
18 台至 19 台，總產能約增加 10%至 20%。新增機台將優先配置於 VCSEL 與 GaSb。
假設工廠平均稼動率保持在 70%至 80%，與 2026 年相近，預估 2027 年營收 16.1
億元(YoY+27.1%)，毛利率在 400G EAM 與 Driver、VCSEL 等高毛利產品營收比重
上升下增至 41.6%，EPS 6.40 元。 
IET-KY 可望成為全球第三家 QD Laser Epi-Wafer 供應商 
展望 2028 年，IET-KY 於 2027 年進入試產階段的 QD Laser GaAs Epi-Wafer 可望開
始放量出貨，成為繼 QD Laser Inc.及 Innolume GmbH 之後，全球第三家 QD Laser 
Epi-Wafer 供應商。200G VCSEL 可望從研發階段步入試產或量產階段。在 InP 部分，
第二位 400G EAM 與 Driver 客戶產品將開始量產，與第一位客戶一同貢獻 InP 營收，
加上 1.6T 之 200G PD 出貨比重提升，預期 InP 營收仍會有雙位數成長。生物辨識所
需的 GaSb Epi-Wafer 則因市場持續擴大而出貨年增雙位數，預估 2028 年營收 19.3
億元(YoY+19.8%)，毛利率 42.4%，EPS 8.16 元。 
MBE 是下一代規格的起源，初次建議區間操作，目標價 424 元 
IET-KY 作為全球唯一一家採用 MBE 製程量產磊晶的供應商，客戶包含多家全球雷射
大廠。在產品規格上，已踏入 400G EAM 與 Driver、200G VCSEL、QD Laser 等高
規格產品所用之 Epi-Wafer 的研發，且 400G EAM 與 Driver、QD Laser 於 2027 年
就會開始出貨，產品進程遠遠領先同業。然而，在 400G 等產品面臨龐大需求時，成
本較低的 MOCVD 勢必取代初期採用的 MBE 成為主要技術，因此 IET-KY 也將維持
「營收規模小、毛利高」的路線，以前沿技術出貨高規格產品而穩定成長。參考光通
訊產業 Forward PE 評價多落在 20X~50X 區間，以 2028 年 EPS 換算，目前為 49X，
評價偏高。初次建議區間操作，目標價 424 元(2028F PE 52X)。

## Document 218: [000660] SK하이닉스(000660) HBM 하나로 시가총액 설명SK하이닉스(000660) HBM 하나로 시가총액 설명HBM 하나로 시가총액 설명 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[000660] SK하이닉스(000660) HBM 하나로 시가총액 설명SK하이닉스(000660) HBM 하나로 시가총액 설명HBM 하나로 시가총액 설명 - 기업.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-11-04
Pages: 12
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

반도체(Overweight): Stress Test  
90 
SK하이닉스  (000660) 
2026.11.04

HBM 하나로 시가총액 설명 
[반도체/전기전자] 고의영 
2122-9179 ey.ko@imfnsec.com

업황 우려에 고유 할인까지 겹친 과도한 하락 
투자의견 Buy, 목표주가 230만원으로 커버리지를 개시한다. ‘27년 BPS에 목표 
P/B 2.3배를 적용했다[표1]. 이는 상장 이후 평균 P/B의 +2표준편차에 해당한다. 
‘26~’28년 ROE는 각각 102%, 66%, 47%로, ‘26년 정점 이후에도 과거 업사이클 
고점보다 높은 수준이 유지될 전망이다.  
한편, 이익 지속성에 대한 믿음이 공고해져 시장의 시야가 ‘28년으로 향할 경우 
‘28년 BPS 기준 목표주가는 360만원까지 열릴 수 있다[표2]. 
동사 주가는 ‘26년 6월 고점 대비 10월 저점 기준 57% 하락해 과거 다운사이클 
평균 하락폭인 -51%를 넘어섰고, 과거 8~13개월에 걸쳐 진행됐던 조정이 
이번에는 4개월 만에 나타났다. 시장이 ‘28년 이익 급감을 상정하고 있는 것이다. 
그러나 당사는 아래와 같은 투자포인트와 향후 주가 흐름을 제시한다.

‘28년 HBM 가치, 시가총액의 1.3배 
HBM 비중이 높아질수록 동사의 이익 변동성은 낮아질 것이다. 특히 cHBM으로 
갈수록 고객별 수주생산 제품으로 현물시장과 유통 재고가 없고, 생산에 앞서 
물량과 가격이 결정된다. 동사는 이 전환이 업계에서 가장 선두권이다.  
당사는 동사 HBM 매출이 ‘26년 43조원에서 ‘28년 183조원으로 증가할 것으로 
전망한다. 만약, 동사의 ‘26~’28년 HBM 영업이익에 시기별 TSMC P/E(21배, 
15배, 12배)를 적용하면 HBM 가치는 동사 시가총액의 47%, 93%, 135%를 
설명한다[표3]. ‘27년에 이미 HBM 하나만으로 현재 시가총액이 설명된다는 
의미다. 시장이 통상적으로 받아들이는 멀티플 10배를 타겟팅해도 결과는 크게 
다르지 않다.

150만원에 사면 ‘30년 EPS 2배 
동사의 주주환원은 자사주 매입/소각 중심일 것으로 상정한다. 당사는 동사가 현 
정책 기간(‘25~’27년)에 이어 ‘28년 이후에도 FCF의 50%를 환원하고 이 중 
70%를 자사주 매입/소각에 쓸 것으로 가정했다.  
당사 가정대로라면 총주주환원수익률은 현 시가총액 대비 ‘27년 14%, ‘28~’30년 
연 17~24%다[표4]. 
관건은 매입 단가다. 자사주 매입의 주당가치 개선 효과는 P/E가 낮을수록 
커지는데, 현 주가의 ‘27년 P/E는 2.7배다. 현 주가 수준인 150만원에 매년 균등 
매입한다고 ‘30년까지 주식수는 50% 줄어든다.   
그 결과 ‘30년 주당순이익은 매입이 없을 때 대비 2배가 된다[표4]. ‘28년 이후 
이익이 정체되더라도 주당 이익은 계속 늘어나는 구조이며, 이익 지속성에 대한 
의심이 주가를 누를수록 소각 효과는 커진다. 현 주가 수준의 정체가 장기화되기 
어려운 이유다.

‘27년 주가 경로: 회복, 박스권, 재평가 
연말까지는 급락 이후 저점을 높여가는 회복 구간이다. 솔리다임 상장 철회에 이어 3분기 
실적발표에서 ‘26년 주주환원 100조원과 매 분기 자사주 매입 방침이 제시되면서 환원 
의지는 검증되었다. 
‘27년 상반기까지는 ‘28년 불확실성에 대한 우려가 상존할 전망이다. 가격 하락폭은 LTA 
방어력이 확인되기 전까지 블랙박스다. 이 불확실성이 멀티플을 누르는 동안 ‘27년 이익은 
계속 상향되므로 EPS 상승과 멀티플 하락이 상쇄되는 구간이 이어질 전망이다. 이 구간의 
기회비용은 환원으로 보상받는다. 
‘27년 하반기 이후에는 실적 추정의 기준 연도가 ‘28년으로 넘어가고, 신규 팹 물량이 소량 
출하되면서 센티먼트상 우려는 커질 것이다. 그러나 당사는 2H27에 ‘28년 HBM 협의와 
LTA 연장이 지속적으로 확인되면서 이익이 스트레스 테스트 범위 안에서 유지될 것으로 
전망하며[그림2], 이것이 숫자로 확인되는 시점에 순환 할인이 축소되고 재평가가 나타날 
것으로 판단한다.

업사이드 요인 
① AI 투자 지속성 우려로 최근 주가 하락이 가팔랐던 만큼, 이 부분에 대한 우려가 
완화되면 반등의 속도와 폭이 예상보다 빠를 수 있다 
② 향후 주주환원 규모가 예상보다 더 적극적일 경우 추가적인 업사이드가 될 수 있다. 
혹은 주주환원 이후에도 남는 대규모 FCF의 활용 방안도 촉매가 될 수 있다.

Compliance notice 
당 보고서 공표일 기준으로 해당 기업과 관련하여, 
 회사는 해당 종목을 1%이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다.  
 금융투자분석사와 그 배우자는 해당 기업의 주식을 보유하고 있지 않습니다.  
 당 보고서는 기관투자가 및 제 3자에게 E-mail등을 통하여 사전에 배포된 사실이 없습니다.  
 회사는 6개월간 해당 기업의 유가증권 발행과 관련 주관사로 참여하지 않았습니다. 
 당 보고서에 게재된 내용들은 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
본 분석자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위한 참고자료이며, 따라서, 본 자료에 의한 투자자의 투자결과에 대해 어떠한 목적의 증빙자료로도 사용될 수 없으며, 어떠한 경우에도 작성자 및 
당사의 허가 없이 전재, 복사 또는 대여될 수 없습니다. 무단전재 등으로 인한 분쟁발생시 법적 책임이 있음을 주지하시기 바랍니다.

[투자의견] 
종목추천 투자등급  
종목투자의견은 향후 12개월간 추천일 종가대비 해당종목의 예상 목표수익률을 의미함.  
 Buy(매수): 추천일 종가대비 +15% 이상  
 Hold(보유): 추천일 종가대비 -15% ~ 15% 내외 등락 
 Sell(매도): 추천일 종가대비 -15% 이상 
산업추천 투자등급  
시가총액기준 산업별 시장비중대비 보유비중의 변화를 추천하는 것임 
 Overweight(비중확대) 
 Neutral(중립) 
 Underweight(비중축소)

## Document 231: [005380] 현대차(005380) 저점을 받쳐줄 실적현대차(005380) 저점을 받쳐줄 실적저점을 받쳐줄 실적 - 기업

투자의견 BUY  
매수, 유지

목표주가 500,000

현재주가 
(26.10.29) 
292,500 
자동차업종

◼ 3Q26 비용절감/환율 효과로 우려 대비 양호한 실적 시현 
◼ 2027년 물량–환율 영향 상쇄되며 2H27 이익체력 개선 본격화 예상 
◼ 1) Physical AI(로봇/SDV) 경쟁력, 2) 주요국 점유율 반등에 주목

투자의견 매수 유지, 목표주가 50만원 하향(-6%) 
현대차 TP 50만원(시총 102조원)은 12MF EPS 41,978원에 Target PER 12배
적용하여 산출. 제한적인 실적 기대 반영해 타겟 밸류 하향함에 따라 TP 조정  
우려 대비 양호한 3Q26 실적으로 주가 하방 부담 완화. 주가의 추세적 상승 
위해서는 1) Physical AI(로봇/SDV) 경쟁력, 2) 주요국 점유율 반등 확인돼야 할 
것. 기저효과에 따른 물량 개선이 주가 저점 받쳐줄 것이나, 현재 밸류(2027년 
PER 9.1배vs 글로벌 피어 6.1배)에서 추세 상승 이끌긴 어려울 것이기 때문. 
이에, 자동차 본업 외 1) 한/미국 Physical AI 투자 계획 및 2) BD 공급망 구축 
/양산 가시화에 따른 주가 반등 기대

3Q26 매출 45조(YoY -3%, 이하 YoY), OP 2.7조(+6%), OPM 5.9% 시현 
3Q26 OP 추정(2.5조)/컨센(2.4조) 상회. 파업에 따른 생산/판매 차질 영향으로 
기대 제한적이었으나, 비용 절감/환율 영향으로 우려 대비 양호한 실적 시현. 
[자동차] 매출 33조(-10%), OP 2.1조(+25%), OPM 6.5%. 도매 판매 92.1만대
(-11%). 판매 부진과 원자재 부담에도 불구, 환효과(+1.3조)/비용절감(+0.5조)
로 실적 방어. 이외 YoY 변수별 영향은 물량(-1.1조)/믹스(-0.6조)/원자재(-
0.5조). [금융] 매출 9.2조(+28%), OP 4,710억(-18%), OPM 5.1%, [기타] 매출 
2.9조(+2%), OP 712억(-72%), OPM 2.5% 기록

2027년 매출 191조(YoY +2%, 이하 YoY), OP 12.7조(+15%), OPM 6.6% 전망 
기저효과 바탕으로 연결 도매 물량 YoY +5% 증가 예상하나, 원달러 환율 -
4% 하락(가정 환율 1,380원)하며 1H27 → 2H27로가며 이익체력 개선 예상

### Page 5

DAISHIN SECURITIES

5  
  현대차 3Q26 실적 컨퍼런스콜 QnA 
Q. 금융부문 환율 변동에 따른 4Q26 매출 및 손익 영향 
- 금융부문 환율 영향은 현대차 실적에 미치는 환율 영향과 동일한 방향으로 발생

Q. 3Q26 금융부문 수익성 악화 요인 
- 장기금리 상승에 따른 조달 및 이자비용 증가가 주요인. 자산/부채 만기 매칭에 따른 
금리 상승 영향 반영 
- EV 중심 중고차 가격지수 소폭 하락 및 차량 반환에 따른 매각손실, 금리 상승에 따
른 연체율 및 신용비용 상승 
- 차량 판매가격 인하 영향은 없으며, 3Q26 도매 판매 감소에 따른 금융 영업수익 영향 
불가피했음

Q. 향후 주주환원 정책에서 배당 및 자사주 매입 우선순위 
- 기존 밸류업 정책: 최소 DPS 1만원/TPR 35% 제시. 연간 배당 약 2.6조원 → TPR 
35% 충족 위한 잔여분 자사주 매입/소각 활용 
- 경영환경 변화 고려하여 기존 밸류업 프로그램 전반의 주주가치 제고 방안 재검토중 
- 새로운 정책 확정 및 주주 소통 전까지 기존 약속인 최소 DPS 1만원 유지할 계획 
- 시장의 배당 및 자사주 매입 간 균형 요구를 인지하고 있으며, 주주 의견 수렴 후 주
주가치 제고 계획 공유 예정

Q. 3Q26 영업이익 증감 요인 중 기타 +6,400억 발생 원인 
- 월별 컨틴전시 활동을 통한 비용 최적화 및 재료비 절감 지속 추진 
- 4Q26 원가 절감 효과는 3Q26 대비 확대 예상

Q. 3Q26 영업외손익 감소 원인 
- 3Q26 환율 급락으로 외화 매출채권 등 외화자산 관련 외화환산손실 및 외환거래손실 
발생 
- 매출채권 인식 시점과 회수 시점 간 환율 차이에 따른 외환차손 발생 
- 지분법 대상 기업들의 3Q26 실적 부진에 따른 지분법손익 감소도 일부 영향. 대부분 
외환 관련 손실에서 발생

Q. 중국 OEM 국내 진출 확대에 따른 대응 및 신차/가격 전략 
- 중국 OEM 및 테슬라의 국내 M/S 확대 대응중. 다만, 잦은 가격 조정은 중고차 잔존
가치 및 고객가치 훼손할 수 있어 지양 
- 현대캐피탈의 캡티브 금융 경쟁력 활용. 잔존가치 유예할부 등을 통해 월 납입금을 낮
춰 중국 OEM 대비 경쟁력 확보 
- 서비스 네트워크 기반으로 경쟁사 대비 경쟁력 확보 노력 
- 국내 신차: 4Q26 아반떼 HEV, 투싼, GV90, G80 HEV, 싼타페 FL(2026.12월), G90 
FL 등 출시 예정 
- 북미: 4Q26 투싼, GV80 HEV 출시 및 27년 초 GV90 출시 예상 
- 유럽: 아이오닉3 판매 개시, 4Q26 투싼/베이온 등 주요 모델 출시 예정 
- 중국: 아이오닉 V / 인도: 베이온, 아이오닉 P 등 출시 예정 
- 신차 출시 계획 연도별로 다수 예정. 가격은 원가/경쟁사/소비자 가격 수용성 등을 고
려해 결정

- 일회성 요인보다는 법인별 손익 믹스 및 적용 세율 차이에 따른 영향 
- 4Q26 법인세 수준은 QoQ 큰 변동 없을 것으로 전망

## Document 249: [207940] 삼성바이오로직스(207940) 단기 부담에도 컨센서스 부합 기대삼성바이오로직스(207940) 단기 부담에도 컨센서스 부합 기대단기 부담_2a8cdc97

Source PDF: all report pdf/[207940] 삼성바이오로직스(207940) 단기 부담에도 컨센서스 부합 기대삼성바이오로직스(207940) 단기 부담에도 컨센서스 부합 기대단기 부담_2a8cdc97.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-06

목표주가 2,000,000

(26.10.02) 
1,354,000 
제약업종

◼ 3Q26 컨센서스 부합, 4Q26 성장폭 확대 전망 
◼ 5공장 Ramp-up 지속, 신규 수주 회복 여부에 주목 
◼ 투자의견 매수, 목표주가 2,000,000원 유지

투자의견 매수, 목표주가 2,000,000원 유지 
목표주가는 DCF 방식으로 산출(WACC 8.1%, 영구성장률 3.5% 적용)

3Q26 Preview: 단기 부담에도 컨센서스 부합 전망 
3Q26 매출액 1조 3,380억원(+6.4% yoy, +1.3% qoq), 영업이익 5,890억원(-
7.0% yoy, +0.4% qoq, OPM 44.0%)으로 컨센서스에 부합하는 실적을 전
망함. 3분기 평균 원/달러 환율이 전분기 대비 약 5% 하락하고, 지난 5월 
파업에 따른 생산 차질 영향이 반영되나, 5공장 PPQ 물량의 매출 인식이 
시작되며 yoy, qoq 외형 성장은 이어질 것으로 예상함.  
파업 당시 생산이 지연된 물량은 3Q 중 추가 생산되어 4Q26부터 매출에 반
영될 전망임. 5공장은 다수 프로젝트의 PPQ가 진행 중이며, Ramp-up에 
따라 매출 기여도 점진적으로 확대될 것으로 예상함. 이에 4Q26에는 파
업 이연 물량 반영과 5공장 매출 확대에 따라 실적 성장폭이 확대될 전
망임. 2026년 연간 매출액과 영업이익은 각각 5조 5,310억원(+21.4% 
yoy), 2조 4,780억원(+19.8% yoy, OPM 44.8%)으로 전망함.

신규 수주 회복 기대, 중장기 성장 기반은 확대 중 
관세 관련 불확실성으로 둔화됐던 고객사와의 수주 논의가 재개되고 있음. 
10월 2일 유럽 소재 제약사와 935억원 규모의 CMO 계약을 추가 체결
했으며, 4Q26 신규 수주 회복 여부에 주목함.  
PolyPeptide 인수는 연내 거래 종결을 목표로 진행 중이며, 인수 완료 
시 펩타이드 분야로 CDMO 포트폴리오가 확대될 전망임. 6공장도 연내 
착공을 목표로 준비 중임. 
한편, 노사 리스크는 단기 불확실성 요인임. 노조는 10/26부터 3주간 2
차 총파업을 예고함. 1차 대비 예정된 파업 기간이 길어진 만큼 실제 파
업 진행 시 생산 차질 규모가 주요 변수임. 다만 파업 개시 전까지 협상 
가능성이 열려 있어, 향후 노사 협상 진행 경과를 확인할 필요가 있음.

## Document 261: [329180] HD현대중공업(329180) 우려 감안해도 충분히 매력적HD현대중공업(329180) 우려 감안해도 충분히 매력적우려 감안해도 충분히 매력적 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[329180] HD현대중공업(329180) 우려 감안해도 충분히 매력적HD현대중공업(329180) 우려 감안해도 충분히 매력적우려 감안해도 충분히 매력적 - 기업.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-14
Pages: 6
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

HD 현대중공업 (329180/KS) 
우려 감안해도 충분히 매력적 
2026-10-14

조선/방산. 한승한, R.A 고서영 / shane.han@sks.co.kr / 3773-9992

매수(유지) 
목표주가: 820,000 원(하향) 
현재주가: 366,500 원 
상승여력: 123.7%

Signal: 3Q26, 조업일수 및 환율 영향으로 시장 예상치 하회 예상 
Key: 4Q26 부터 본격적인 발전엔진 및 FDC 계약 수주 모멘텀 기대 
Step: 상선 우려 감안해도 4 행정 엔진&FDC 으로 주가 상승여력 
충분. 매수

3Q26 Preview: 컨센서스 하회하는 실적 기록 예상 
3Q26 연결 매출액 6조 464억원(+36.9% YoY, -4.5% QoQ), 영업이익 9,230억
원(+65.6% YoY, -11.2% QoQ)으로, 시장 예상치(1조 70억원)를 하회하는 실적 기
록할 것으로 추정한다. 이전 분기 대비 중대재해 및 하계휴가, 그리고 추석 연휴에 따른 
조업일수 감소 효과와 더불어 큰 폭의 환율 하락 영향(약 500 억원) 때문이다. 홍범도
함 화재 및 임단협 관련 일회성 비용은 이번 분기가 아닌 4Q 에 반영될 것으로 예상한
다. 해양 부문에서 트리온 FPU 프로젝트 C/O 에 따른 일부 환입 발생한 것으로 추정
되며, 해양 도크에서 컨테이너선 선체 블록 일부 건조로 고정비 감소효과 일부 상쇄 중
이다. 다만 올해 연내 혹은 내년 초 추가 해양 수주 없을 시 고정비 상승 우려 확대될 
전망이다. 동사 올해 상선 수주 목표(114.7 억불)를 이미 초과하는 수주 실적 기록한 상
황이며, 고마진 선종인 LNGC 및 VLGC/VLAC 중심으로 수주했기 때문에 ‘28~’29
년 구간 경쟁사 대비 뚜렷한 선종 믹스 효과를 기대한다.

투자의견 ‘매수’ 유지, 목표주가 82 만원으로 하향 
HD 현대중공업에 대한 투자의견 ‘매수’를 유지하고, 목표주가는 기존 97 만원에서 82
만원으로 하향한다. 중국의 공격적인 캐파 증설 지속과 더불어 중국 대형 조선사들의 
글로벌 선주가 발주한 LNGC 물량 및 원가 경쟁력 확대에 따른 상선 부문의 멀티플 
조정이 불가피하다는 판단이다. 하지만 상선 부문의 우려를 감안하더라도 상선 사이클
과 무관한 육상 발전 엔진 및 FDC에 대한 글로벌 하이퍼스케일러들의 수요 확대는 지
속되고 있는 중으로 파악된다. 올해 4 분기부터 4 행정 중속엔진&FDC 부문의 본격적
인 수주를 통해 중장기적 신성장 동력 확보할 전망이며, 해당 모멘텀에 따른 주가 상승
여력 충분히 남아있다는 판단으로, 조선주 최선호주(Top-Pick)를 유지한다.

## Document 270: AI is boosting business creations both in the US and Europe (2026-11-04)

Source PDF: all report pdf/AI is boosting business creations both in the US and Europe (2026-11-04).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-11-04
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

4 November 2026
The bank
for a changing
world
AI IS BOOSTING BUSINESS CREATIONS BOTH IN THE US AND EUROPE
Stéphane Colliac
The significant increase in business creations since mid-2025 in the United States, France and Germany – and, to a lesser extent, in the United
Kingdom – is continuing. Across the board, this trend is being driven by the information and communication sector and mostly by AI. It is
spreading to other business services, while developments in consumer services, industry and construction are more mixed. However, in both

the United States and France, the trend is broadening, suggesting that AI is facilitating business creation across all sectors.
Business creations by sector: a boom that is building momentum and spreading

We have calculated the year-on-year change in the number of new business start-ups. The table categorises these results as positive (red), neutral (white) or negative (blue). The darker
the colour, the larger the change. The data series are defined as follows: Germany = new entries in the companies register (start-ups) since 2008; France = business start-ups, including

micro-entrepreneurs, since 2000; United Kingdom = businesses registered for VAT/PAYE since 2017; United States = business applications (tax identification number) since 2004. Sectors
have been grouped together to ensure that the classification is standardised across all countries. For the third quarter, the German data only include the months of July and August.

### Page 2

2
The bank for
a changing
world
Chart of the Week / 4 November 2026 economic-research.bnpparibas.com
A noticeable boom in the United States, France, and Germany, but not quite as pronounced in the United Kingdom
The four economies for which we have comparable data (the United States, France, Germany and the United Kingdom) have all
seen, to varying degrees, an increase in the number of new business creations. While the timing varies across these countries,

most of them are enjoying strong momentum. In the United States, there has been a noticeable acceleration since the second
quarter of 2025, which has continued to strengthen. Germany has followed a similar trajectory, moving from slight declines at
the start of 2024 to sharp increases. France is experiencing the same trend but with some specific features, as momentum has
picked up significantly after months of slowdown (due to the uncertainty following the dissolution of the National Assembly) and is

accelerating across most sectors in 2026. In the United Kingdom, the trend is less pronounced and the increase may not last.
Business services, and AI in particular, are driving this momentum
In most of these four countries, business creations are rising significantly in the information and communication sector, which is
directly linked to AI. In the United States, Germany and France, there has been a pronounced trend which is gaining momentum.
In the United Kingdom, the AI sector is also seeing an increase, but it is significantly less pronounced. However, this momentum is

not limited to this sector. It is spreading to many services sectors, including finance, real estate and business services. Growth has
been sustained since mid-2025 in the United States and Germany, and since the third quarter of 2025 in France.
The picture for consumer services is more mixed. In the United States, trade, accommodation and catering have experienced major
increases in creations since mid-2025. In France, these sectors are in positive territory, particularly trade, where the increase in

business creations has recently accelerated. In Germany, these same sectors fell into negative territory in 2026 following a rather
mixed year in 2025.
The situation is even more mixed for manufacturing and construction. In the United States, these sectors are also part of the
overall positive momentum in the third quarter of 2026. This shows that AI does not only benefit sectors closely linked to it
(notably the digital technology sector) but that it makes it easier to start a business across the entire economy (by simplifying

administrative procedures in particular). In France, the upturn in business creations in the construction sector suggests that this
same trend is at play, even as activity in the sector continues to contract.
Therefore, the trend is more widespread in the United States and is becoming increasingly prevalent in France. It is driven by
specific sectors of the services industry in Germany, while it is significantly more subdued in the United Kingdom.

stephane.colliac@bnpparibas.com
Article written with the help of Ece Usta (intern).

### Page 2

Weekly Economics
U.S. Week Ahead
Existing Home Sales • Thursday
Housing market conditions remain highly unfavorable. Mortgage
rates are the primary headwind, and they have climbed even higher
alongside the rise in longer-term yields. At the same time, low
supply is preventing a broad correction in home prices, leaving
prospective buyers squeezed on two fronts.
We expect that existing home sales slid 3.4% in August to an
annualized rate of 3.92 million. Behind this forecast is ongoing

weakness in mortgage applications and a recent bounce in
mortgage rates, which rose from 6.43% at the beginning of July to
6.66% by month-end.
We do not expect affordability conditions to meaningfully improve
in the near term. Supply is likely to remain constrained while high
mortgage rates discourage mobility. And mortgage rates are apt
to remain elevated amid firm economic growth expectations and
heavy government and corporate debt issuance.
(Return)

### Page 3

Economics Week Ahead Economics
CPI • Friday
Headline inflation looks to have picked up last month. We estimate
the CPI rose 0.40% in August, with gasoline prices increasing a little
over 4% as renewed tensions in the Middle East pushed oil higher.
Grocery prices also likely rebounded after modestly declining in July.
Outside of food and energy, price pressures appear little changed.
We expect the core CPI to rise 0.23%, essentially matching July's

pace. Core goods inflation should remain firm, led by another solid
increase in used vehicle prices as consumer prices continue to
catch up to the trend indicated at wholesale auctions. Meantime,
household furnishings and other tariff-sensitive categories appear
to be stabilizing after declining earlier this year.
Core services inflation should be little changed with another 0.2%
monthly increase. But underneath the surface, we expect a modest
pickup in travel (led by lodging away from home) to offset slightly

cooler medical care and housing cost growth.
All told, we expect the report to show that while headline inflation
remains influenced by the ebbs and flows of the conflict in the
Middle East, core inflation remains contained. Should our forecast
prove correct, core CPI will ease to 2.4% year-over-year, in line with
its 6-month annualized rate but above the more recent 3-month
annualized pace of 1.7%.

Economics | 3

### Page 4

Weekly Economics
G10 Week Ahead
European Central Bank Monetary Policy Meeting •
Thursday
We expect the European Central Bank (ECB) to raise the Deposit
Rate by 25 bps to 2.50% next week. August headline inflation
remained elevated at 3.3% year-over-year, though softer core
and services inflation suggest underlying pressures continue to
moderate. Still, the more hawkish tone of the July minutes and
renewed rise in energy prices should keep policymakers cautious.

The recent escalation in the Middle East has pushed fuel prices
at the pump close to their 2022 peaks, while natural gas storage
remains below historical levels, leaving the Eurozone vulnerable
to further energy price increases. The ECB is unlikely to respond
aggressively if the shock remains confined to energy given the
downside risks to growth. However, a broader rise in food inflation
linked to El Niño would be more concerning, as highly visible prices

could lift inflation expectations and generate second-round effects.
For now, resilient Q3 activity, elevated headline inflation and
contained wage growth support one final hike. The ECB will likely
retain and emphasize its scenario-based outlook in the updated
staff projections given continued uncertainty around the conflict
and energy prices. The policy path beyond September will depend
on whether energy and food pressures persist and begin to spread,

which could leave room for further tightening.

U.K. Monthly GDP • Friday
We expect UK GDP to rise 0.2% month-over-month in July, with
growth of 0.3% on a three-month-over-three-month basis.

Activity likely moderated after June’s strong rebound, though
services remained supportive as the July services PMI rose to 52.1.
Retail sales volumes fell 0.5% month-over-month, while softer
manufacturing activity likely reflected some reversal of earlier
precautionary stockbuilding.
The moderation appears more consistent with payback from
June than a broader deterioration in momentum. August PMIs
remain consistent with Q3 GDP growth of around 0.3% quarter-

over-quarter. Still, the renewed escalation in the Middle East and
associated rise in gas and energy prices adds upside risk to inflation
and could weigh on household demand. Against this backdrop and
with Bank of England hawks continuing to emphasize the need for
tighter policy, we expect the BoE to remain cautious and continue
to look for a rate hike in Q4. -1.0%

### Page 5

Economics Week Ahead Economics
EM Week Ahead
Mexico CPI • Wednesday
We expect Mexico’s August inflation rate to edge higher, with
headline CPI rising to 3.34% year-over-year (0.27% month-over-
month) from 3.26% year-over-year in early August. Part of the
pickup likely reflects less favorable base effects and a smaller
disinflationary contribution from food prices. While agricultural
prices continued to help restrain inflation in mid-August, the drag

was notably less pronounced than in July. Core inflation should
remain elevated at 3.98% year-over-year (0.25% month-over-
month).
While inflation remains within Banxico’s midpoint 3% target,
persistent core and services inflation suggest convergence toward
the midpoint of the target could prove gradual. For now, we
continue to expect Banxico to keep rates on hold, with the policy
rate remaining at 6.50% through the rest of 2026 and into early
2027 as policymakers await clearer evidence that underlying

inflation pressures are easing sustainably. While rate cuts remain
a possibility in 2027, the current inflation backdrop argues for
patience in the near term.

Brazil CPI • Friday
We expect Brazil’s August inflation rate to slow to 4.20% year-over-
year, helped by lower electricity, food and fuel prices. The main
driver should be a temporary reduction in residential electricity bills
stemming from a one-off credit, while recent data already point to
a notable easing in price pressures. Mid-August CPI came in weaker
than expected, with inflation falling to 4.24% year-over-year amid
declines in electricity, food, fuel and airfare prices. Beyond these

temporary factors, restrictive monetary policy continues to weigh
on economic activity, as evidenced by the recent slowdown in Q2
GDP growth. However, underlying inflation risks remain. A still-
tight labor market and government measures aimed at supporting
consumption could keep services inflation sticky, making progress
toward the 3.0% inflation target more gradual.
Overall, the inflation outlook remains consistent with additional
policy easing. We expect COPOM to deliver a 25 bps rate cut in

September, bringing the Selic Rate to 13.75%. Beyond September,
policymakers are likely to proceed cautiously as they assess
incoming inflation data and the impact of the election cycle on
inflation expectations. Our base case remains for a pause after
September, with additional easing more likely in 2027.

## Document 364: Jeffries连任众议院民主党领袖，明年1月有望成为美国首位黑人众议长

Source PDF: all report pdf/Jeffries连任众议院民主党领袖，明年1月有望成为美国首位黑人众议长.pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 2
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

据媒体报道，⺠主党众院领袖 Hakeem Jeffries 以 251 ⽐ 11 的压倒性票数蝉联党内领袖，并
有望于明年 1 ⽉成为美国史上⾸位⿊⼈众议院议⻓。⺠主党此次中期选举预计斩获逾 260
席，创本世纪最⼤众院多数优势，这⼀宽裕席位优势使⺠主党在关税、社会安全⽹等议题
上具备向⽩宫强⼒施压的底⽓。
摘要：
⺠主党众院领袖 Hakeem Jeffries 以压倒性优势赢得连任，在党内中期选举⼤捷的背景下，他有
望于明年 1 ⽉成为美国历史上⾸位⿊⼈众议院议⻓。
据彭博援引⼀名与会⼈⼠透露，Jeffries 在秘密投票中获得 251 票，仅有 11 票反对，以悬殊优势
稳固了党内⽀持。
据新华社华， 11 ⽉ 18 ⽇，美国众议院⺠主党党团提名现任众议院⺠主党领袖哈基姆 · 杰弗⾥斯为
下届众议院议⻓，使他有望成为美国历史上⾸位⾮洲裔众议院议⻓。
明年 1 ⽉，众议院将就新议⻓⼈选进⾏全院投票， Jeffries 普遍被认为能够获得所需的 218 票。⺠
主党此次中期选举预计将斩获⾄少 260 个席位，为本世纪任何政党中最⼤的众院多数优势。

Jeffries 在胜选后的新闻发布会上表⽰，新⼀届⺠主党多数将聚焦降低⺠众⽣活成本 —— 尤其是
医疗⽀出 —— 并着⼒反腐。这⼀施政重⼼，配合其党内近乎团结的局⾯，使⺠主党在四年共和
党掌权之后，有望就关税政策、社会安全⽹削减等优先议题向⽩宫施压。
历史性时刻：从布鲁克林到众议院第⼀把交椅
Jeffries 的当选被多位⺠主党⼈视为历史性⾥程碑。
南卡罗来纳州众议员 Jim Clyburn 在出席闭⻔党团会议时援引已故⺠权运动领袖 John Lewis 的精
神，称其为⿊⼈美国⼈的重要⾥程碑。 "John Lewis 的艰⾟努⼒终于得到了回报， "Clyburn 表
⽰。
Jeffries 于 2012 年⾸次当选国会议员，代表布鲁克林及皇后区部分地区的强⺠主党选区。
在进⼊国会之前，他曾在纽约州议会任职，并有私⼈律所及维亚康姆、哥伦⽐亚⼴播公司内部
法律顾问的从业经历。 2023 年，他接替前议⻓ Nancy Pelosi 出任⺠主党众院领袖，此前曾是众
议院进步派核⼼成员。
党内团结与进步派压⼒并存

尽管 Jeffries ⾯临进步派的⼀定批评，认为他在推动 " 全⺠医保 " 等政策上⼒度不⾜，但本次选举
中⽆⼈挑战其领导地位。
七名新当选的⺠主党众议员发表联名声明，表⽰因对 Jeffries 在全⺠医保、加沙停⽕及移⺠执法
等问题上的⽴场不满⽽投票反对，但同时承认双⽅在谈判中已取得 " 实质性进展 " ， " 仍有⼯作需
要推进 " 。
Jeffries 上周宣布，将把降低 Medicare 享受资格年龄、并将⽛科、听⼒及视⼒覆盖纳⼊该计划，
作为迈向 " 全⺠医保 " 的第⼀步。这⼀表态在⼀定程度上向进步派释放信号，同时保持了与党内温
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

⺠主党庞⼤多数席位筑起 " 护城河 "

明对⽐ —— 前任共和党议⻓ Kevin McCarthy 因党内极保守派借助微弱多数反复施压，历经 15 轮

党领袖，升⾄第⼆号位置；加利福尼亚州的 Pete Aguilar 将担任负责票数统计的多数党督导。
相⽐之下，参议院⺠主党领袖 Chuck Schumer 则⾯临来⾃⻢⾥兰州⺠主党⼈ Chris Van Hollen 的
领导层挑战，⺠主党两院的政治⽣态由此呈现出截然不同的⾯貌。

近三月(%)
► 美國10月CPI與核心CPI月增率均高於市場預
期，推升市場對Fed進一步升息的預估。加
上Fed理事Waller表示，若核心CPI月增率維
持0.3%或更高，12月應考慮升息兩碼，相關
消息同步推升美國公債殖利率，並帶動整體
債券市場跌多漲少。
► 原先市場擔憂期中選舉後川普將再度攻擊伊
朗，然隨近期川普一度淡化對伊朗發動軍事
攻擊的可能性，並稱戰爭已實質結束，市場
因而下修中東地緣政治溢價，並帶動布蘭特
油價大幅回落至每桶90美元附近。惟川普於
週間再度指稱不需與伊朗達成協議，短時間
態度反覆變化令市場對美伊談判疑慮再起，
預料油價短期將呈現區間震盪。
► 受市場強化聯準會升息預期支撐，帶動美元
指數呈現強勁上漲，並同步拖累歐元與日圓
顯著走弱，其中美元兌日圓匯價已逼近160
之整數關卡，短期恐引發干預措施的預期，
走勢料偏向震盪。

► 受能源價格增速放緩影響，美國10月CPI月
增率由前值0.6%降至0.4%，核心CPI月增率
維持0.3%水準，惟整體表現高於預期，其中
食品、住房及運輸服務持續上漲，顯示核心
通膨相 對具 有黏 性 ，物價 壓力未 見全 面緩
解；此外，因去年聯邦政府關門造成統計缺
漏，故本次不公布年增率數值。考量美國就
業市場表現強勁，通膨降溫進程不如預期，
疊加近期聯準會官員偏鷹發言，強化市場押
注年底升息一碼之預期。
► 隨民主黨拿下參眾兩院，美伊重啟和談訊號
浮現，市場對戰事降溫抱持正向期待，帶動
國際油價一度自高檔回落；此外，受2026年
油價高基期影響，若油價未再進一步出現大
幅攀升，預估2027年原油價格年增速可望趨

於收斂，對於整體通膨的推升力道亦有望減
緩。美國方面，受惠於頁岩油生產效率提升
及墨西哥灣新離岸油田陸續投產，則可望帶
動美國原油產量持續成長。

### Page 6

6
投資焦點
► 近期美股市場風格加速轉換，隨著市場更加
重視AI商業化成效與投資回報，高估值成長
股波動顯著升溫。相較之下，價值股則逐漸
受到市場青睞，尤其是憑藉穩定現金流、健
康資產負債表與較高股東回報率之高品質價
值股，除了擁有價值優勢之外，更具備更好
防禦力與抗震能力。
► 從風險維度進行觀察，近10年股市面臨3次
升息循環，不論是2016年、2022年甚至是
2026年9月啟動之升息，高品質價值股指數
之90天波動度平均值皆低於成長股與大盤指
數，顯示若將具備穩定獲利、自由現金流與
較低財 務槓 桿之 高 品質價 值股納 入投 資組
合，有助降低市場修正期間的波動風險。
► 長期而言，高品質價值股的累積報酬率雖不
及成長股在景氣擴張期的爆發力，但仍具優
於大盤的累積報酬潛力，代表在市場波動加
劇、利率升高的環境下，其兼具防禦與長期
複利之優勢已然是長期投資不可或缺關鍵。
高品質價值股長期報酬優於大盤價值股相對成長股獲市場青睞，波動度亦相對偏低
成長股短線波動大，留意價值股表現機會

### Page 7

7
投資焦點國防股
► 國防股通常具備穩定攀升之自由現金流，主
要受惠於長期政府合約與產業高進入門檻所
支持。相較景氣循環股，國防產業獲利與現
金流波動幅度較低，使其兼具一定程度的防
禦性與長期價值配置優勢。
► 北美國防企業近年受惠於地緣政治風險升溫
與軍備補庫存需求，訂單能見度明顯提升。
包含飛彈、防空系統、軍機及潛艦等關鍵裝
備，有助維持歷史高檔積壓訂單水位。龐大
訂單庫存不僅支撐未來數年營收成長，也有
助降低短期景氣波動對企業基本面的影響。
► 根據SIPRI 資料，2025 年 全 球 軍 事 支 出 達
2.89兆美元，已連續11年成長，全球軍事支
出占GDP比重升至2.5%，創2009年以來新
高。其中歐洲軍費年增14%、亞洲與大洋洲
年增 8.1%。在美伊衝突、區域安全風險及北
約提高國防支出目標等因素帶動下，各國軍
事現代化需求可望延續，皆將為國防產業提
供長期且具結構性的成長動能。
地緣政治問題未解，全球軍事支出持續增加國防產業現金流穩定攀升，積壓訂單成長強勁
現金流穩定，補庫存預期支撐後續國防股走勢

### Page 8

8
股市
 黃仁勳相關事件恐逐步強化美國對AI晶片出口管制、AI資料中心建設進程或將受到州政府緊縮政策
而造成評價面壓力並增添股價波動。短期可留意防禦股以增強抗震力以抵禦市場震盪。長期而言，
AI從全面看好轉向選股，且大優於小，市場波瀾過後產業長期正向趨勢未變。當前則可布局財務健
全度較高的高品質價值股，以獲取穩定報酬並降低市場波動影響。
 歐、日股可分散配置，利率走升有利銀行股。此外，隨財政政策支持，歐洲國防軍工仍有投資題材。
策略風格：短期可增加防禦股配置，AI從
全面看好轉向選股、且大優於小，市場波
瀾後長期正向趨勢未變，當前可布局財務
健全度較高的高品質價值股
區域：日本銀行股、歐洲國防
債市
 美伊談判走向反覆未明，油價維持高檔震盪。10月CPI高於預期，強化市場對聯準會升息步伐預估，
聯準會官員立場亦偏向維持較高利率水平抑制通膨發展。建議以中短天期債券投資為主，可逢利率
走升時布局進行鎖利領息。高信評債體質較佳，具穩定現金流與還款能力，聚焦風險調整後利差具
吸引力者，包含金融、通訊及公用事業等產業。

債種：中短天期高信評債鎖利，產業如金
融、通訊及公用事業等
匯市
 10月CPI數據支撐升息預期，美元走勢偏強。
 日本銀行9月升息，但前瞻指引偏中性，未如市場預期偏鷹，日圓弱勢盤整。
美元高檔整理
商品
 美伊談判走向反覆，油價一度回落但再度反彈，短線仍偏震盪。
 去美元化支撐全球央行買盤，提供黃金中長期穩定支撐。
低接買盤流入，黃金低點反彈，中長期維
持偏多看法
類別 市場看法 偏好資產
策略思維
策略思維 短期AI相關變數持續發酵，高品質價值股有望獲得青睞

個別客戶因年齡、投資能力、金融商品交易經驗及風險承受度不同，適合之服務及/或金融商品亦有不同，應進一步與專屬理財業務人員進行評估。投資人應徹底瞭解相關服務及金融商品之風險，並審慎評估個人財力、資產狀況、
理財需求及風險承受能力，獨立判斷並自行決定是否投資及承擔有關風險。投資任何商品皆具有風險，商品以往之績效不保證未來之投資收益，本公司不負責商品之盈虧，也不保證最低之收益。
本公司保留對本文件及其內容之著作權及其他相關權利，任何人未經本公司同意，不得翻印或作其他任何用途使用(包括但不限於傳送或轉發予其他人士等)。如有違反情事，本公司除不負任何責任外，亦將對未經同意之使用與散布
等行為究責。
基金警語
投資一定有風險，基金投資有賺有賠，申購前應詳閱公開說明書
【信託產品投資須知及風險預告：（請投資人審慎評估下列各項條文內容）】
1. 基金（包括具有定期配息之基金）投資具投資風險，此一風險可能使信託本金發生全部虧損。
2. 基金委託人須自負盈虧，本公司受託投資不保本不保息，除盡善良管理人注意義務外，投資所可能產生的本金虧損、匯率損失、或基金解散、清算、移轉、合併等風險，均由委託人承擔。

3. 本公司受託投資之基金經金管會核准或同意生效，惟不表示絕無風險。基金經理公司以往之經理績效不保證基金之最低投資收益；基金經理公司除盡善良管理人之注意義務外，不負責基金之盈虧，亦不保證最低之收益，委託人
申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書。
4. 投資前應詳閱風險預告及各該基金公開說明書，確實作好財務規劃與風險評估。本公司受託投資之基金已備有公開說明書(或其中譯本)或投資人須知【前述文件併揭露有關基金應負擔之費用(境外基金含分銷費用)】，委託人可
至本公司營業處所索取或至公開資訊觀測站、基金資訊觀測站或本公司網站下載。
5. 為保護既有投資人之權益，部份境外基金設有價格稀釋調整機制及公平市價規定，投資人申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書及投資人須知。
6. 委託人瞭解並同意受託人辦理特定金錢信託業務(即委託人委託投資基金)交易時，自交易相對人取得之報酬、費用、折讓等各項利益，得作為受託人收取之信託報酬；另委託人在持有基金期間，受託人仍持續收受經理費分成報
酬，委託人可至受託人(即本公司)網站 (https://www.kgi.com.tw/zh-tw/)查詢基金之通路報酬。其基金經理費率及其分成費率與其他通路報酬最新變動情形，亦可至該網站查詢。

7. 後收型基金在贖回時，基金公司將依持有期間長短收取不同比率之遞延申購手續費，該費用將自贖回總額中扣除；另後收型基金若有收取分銷費，則申購手續費雖可遞延收取，惟每年仍需支付0%~1.06%的分銷費（按基金日平
均淨資產價值計算），可能造成實際負擔費用增加（分銷費依各基金公司而有不同，請參考本公司後收型基金投資費用暨特約事項及各基金之投資人須知），另該費用已反映於每日基金淨值中，委託人無需額外支付。
8. 委託人之交易如經基金公司認定為短線交易者，基金公司或本公司可拒絕受理申請轉換或申購要求，並可請求支付一定比例之贖回費用或其他相關費用，費用標準均依各該基金公司之規定。
9. 委託人因不同時間進場，將有不同之投資績效，過去之績效亦不代表未來績效之保證。基金配息率不代表基金報酬率，且過去配息率不代表未來配息率；基金淨值可能因市場因素而上下波動，投資人於獲配息時，宜一併注意基
金淨值之變動。又投資新興市場可能比投資已開發國家有較大的價格波動及流動性較低的風險，委託人應慎選投資標的。
10. 部分基金可能持有衍生性商品部位，該部位可能超過基金淨資產價值之100%，並可能因而造成基金淨值高度波動及衍生其他風險。

11. 基金的配息可能由基金的收益或本金中支付。任何涉及由本金支出的部分，可能導致原始投資金額減損；且部分基金配息前未先扣除應負擔之費用。由本金支付配息之相關資料，投資人可至證券投資信託事業或總代理人之公司
網站查詢。
12. 非投資等級債券基金（本類基金主要係投資於非投資等級之高風險債券且配息來源可能為本金）（主要特色係投資前揭基金之組合基金亦同），適合能承受較高風險之『非保守型』委託人且不宜占投資組合過高的比重。由於非
投資等級債券之信用評等未達投資等級或未經信用評等，且對利率變動的敏感度甚高，可能會因利率上升、市場流動性下降，或債券發行機構違約不支付本金、利息或破產而蒙受虧損，不適合無法承擔相關風險之委託人；又如
係本國投信機構募集者，則可能投資於符合美國Rule 144A規定具有私募性質之債券，雖其投資總金額不得超過基金淨資產價值之百分之三十，然該債券具有債券發行人違約之信用風險、利率風險、流動性風險、交易對手風險
及因財務訊息揭露不完整或因價格不透明導致波動性較大之風險，投資人應審慎評估；另證券投資信託事業募集之債券型基金、平衡型基金、不動產證券化基金、以追蹤、模擬或複製標的指數表現之指數股票型基金（ETF）及

指數型基金或募集之基金投資新興市場國家債券達基金淨資產價值之百分之六十以上者，或有可能投資符合美國Rule 144A規定具有私募性質之債券，亦應留意相關風險。
13. 投資於中國之基金應遵守中華民國相關法令之規定：境外基金(不含經金管會核准向證交所申請上市或交易之境外ETF)如有投資大陸地區之有價證券者，以掛牌上市有價證券及銀行間債券市場為限，且投資前述有價證券總金額
不得超過該境外基金淨資產價值之百分之二十(境外基金機構依鼓勵境外基金深耕計畫向金管會申請並經認可者得適用鼓勵境外基金深耕計畫所列放寬前揭投資總金額上限至百分之四十之優惠措施)；證券投資信託基金雖可投資
大陸地區之有價證券，惟仍應符合信託契約、公開說明書、證券投資信託基金管理辦法等相關規定及受到基金公司QFII額度之限制。又投資人須留意中國市場之政府政策、法令、會計稅務制度、經濟與市場等變動所可能產生之
投資風險。
14. 各基金於公開（含簡式）說明書或投資人須知揭露之風險報酬等級，係依據投信投顧公會「基金風險報酬等級分類標準」而訂定，本公司亦依據投信投顧公會「基金風險報酬等級分類標準」訂定基金產品風險等級。

富管理客服專線或透過財富管理客服信箱服務，查詢申訴案件處理進度。

### Page 2

Standard Chartered Bank

Charts of the week: Watching earnings and a potential ‘Blue wave’

US Big Tech earnings, mid-term elections and major central bank policy meetings are the near-term market drivers 
Consensus S&P500 index Q3 earnings estimates by sector   Chance of Democrats winning control of US House and Senate*

Source: Bloomberg, Standard Chartered; *Polymarket probability

Editorial 
Policy, politics and earnings  
Strategy summary: We retain our near-term defensive and 
diversified stance across assets, sectors and regions amid 
near-term uncertainty around monetary policy, politics and 
earnings. The next two weeks will be busy for major central 
banks, US political watchers and technology leaders powering 
the AI supercycle. We expect the Fed, ECB and BoJ to hold 
rates next week, but markets will focus on whether central 
banks are turning more hawkish due to the latest surge in fuel 
prices. The US mid-term elections are expected to give 
Democrats control of the House and Senate, with implications 
for taxes and the AI-led investment boom.  
Meanwhile, the consensus is expecting the US tech sector to 
report over 60% y/y earnings growth for Q3, the biggest jump 
in over two decades outside the post-COVID rebound.  Each of 
these three drivers have the potential to surprise markets.  
Big Three to hold monetary policy: We expect the Fed, ECB 
and BoJ to hold policy rates next week, after September’s 
hikes, as they assess whether higher bond yields have 
tightened financial conditions enough to offset fuel-price-driven 
inflation. We expect the Big Three to hike next in December as 
fuel prices are likely to stay elevated into early next year due to 
refining capacity constraints. Nevertheless, the Fed and ECB 
are likely to deliver less than around 60bps of rate hikes priced 
in by money markets over the next 12 months on the back of 
an eventual easing of oil and fuel prices leading to disinflation 
by H2 2027. This outlook means 3-7-year Developed Market 
investment grade bonds currently offer attractive risk-reward.  
Opportunity in Australian bonds. Other major central banks, 
such as the Bank of England, Bank of Canada and Reserve 
Bank of New Zealand are likely to hike rates by 25bps in the 
next two weeks given their focus on taming near-term inflation. 
However, we believe the Reserve Bank of Australia (RBA) is 
close to the end of its hiking cycle, even if it hikes one last time 
in the coming months (money markets give a 60% chance of a 
hike on 3 November). Australia’s domestic labour and housing 
markets and business confidence are softening after the RBA 
hiked its policy rate four times this year to a 15-year high. 
Meanwhile, Australia’s cash rate is 60bps higher than the US 
policy rate, supporting the AUD. We continue to see attractive 
income and FX gain opportunities from AUD corporate bonds.  
A ‘Blue Wave’ in the US? Prediction markets strongly favour 
Democrats taking the House of Representatives and possibly 
the Senate in the 3 November US mid-term elections. While an 
opposition sweep in the mid-terms has historically delivered the 
best 12-month returns for equities among all scenarios, partly 
due to the resulting policy gridlock, a decisive Democratic “blue 
wave” could increase longer-term risks of higher taxes and 
uncertainty over policy towards AI investment and data centres. 
With the Senate race still close and prediction markets fallible, 
we remain cautious on risk assets in the near term.  
Closing EU bank contingent convertible bonds (CoCos1) 
and US 10-year government bond ideas: We lock in 2% 
gains in EU CoCos and 1.7% gain in US 10-year government 
bond ideas amid French and US political risks (page 5-6). We 
continue to hedge any inflation risks through inflation-protected 
bonds and geopolitical risk through an Overweight in gold.  
Watching Big Tech earnings:  Upcoming Big Tech earnings 
raises uncertainty as market leadership remains concentrated 
and share-price reactions more mixed. With valuations less 
likely to drive returns, earnings delivery and sustainability of AI 
investment, funding and monetisation and broadening out of 
earnings beyond tech will be critical, raising the risk of volatility 
and sector divergence. We remain invested but defensively 
positioned, favouring diversification across sectors and regions.  
Staying defensive and diversified. We recently upgraded the 
defensive US Healthcare sector to Overweight, while retaining 
our Overweights to US tech and materials as well as financials 
in the US, Euro area and Japan. We also downgraded the more 
volatile Communications Services sector to Neutral and took 
profits on the semiconductor segment earlier this month before 
the latest correction. Our defensive and diversified stance 
should preserve participation in further equity upside, while 
reducing exposure to earnings disappointments and supporting 
resilience through the reporting season.  
—  Rajat Bhattacharya

### Page 4

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 23 October 2026

Top client questions 
  What are the key takeaways from the technology sector 
earnings reported so far this week? 
Our view: Recent technology sector earnings results point to 
strong compute demand and continued capex expansion, 
supporting our positive long-term view on the AI investment 
theme. However, rising funding and execution risks warrant 
near-term caution. We prefer to avoid increasing exposure to 
higher-risk areas within the technology sector for now. 
Rationale: Earnings results reported this week suggest major tech 
companies remain committed to expanding their AI infrastructure. 
While this bodes well for AI infrastructure providers, ambitious 
expansion plans imply that an increasing share of AI capex is likely 
to depend on debt financing. We expect debt-financed AI capex to 
rise from c.25% of AI capex in 2026 to c.40% in 2027.  
Greater reliance on borrowing increases sensitivity to interest rates, 
while deployment delays could postpone revenue recognition amid 
ongoing financing costs. However, evidence of improving AI 
monetisation and adoption should strengthen operating cash 
flows and support internal capex funding. Against this backdrop, 
we will assess Big Tech earnings next week for greater clarity on 
capex guidance and funding and AI monetisation trends.  
—  Ryan Goh, Investment Strategist 
  Are you still positive on Euro area bank equities amid 
heightened political and fiscal uncertainty in the region? 
Our view: We remain positive on Euro area bank equities, 
supported by resilient earnings and shareholder-friendly capital 
return policies. While France’s fiscal uncertainty and a softer 
growth outlook warrant caution, the recent pullback in Euro 
area bank equities offers selective opportunities. We favour 
well-capitalised banks with strong deposit franchises and 
diversified earnings. 
Rationale: We expect Euro area banks’ Q3 2026 net interest 
income to grow 1.5-3.0% q/q, led by Spanish banks, driven by 
higher rates and continued asset repricing. Resilient fee income from 
wealth management and investment banking activity, as well as 
dividends and buybacks, should provide further support. Price-to-
book (P/B) valuations have eased, despite expectations for 
healthy return on equity (ROE), thus improving risk-reward. 
Fiscal uncertainty in France has widened sovereign spreads, 
pressuring banks’ bond holdings and funding costs. Although 
French banks represent 13% of the Euro area Bank Index, volatility 
could spread to regional financial markets. However, stress in the 
broader banking system appears limited, supported by strong 
capital buffers and ECB tools to contain disorderly market moves and 
sovereign market contagion. We will continue to monitor credit 
trends, sovereign spreads and banks’ 2027 earnings guidance. 
—  Robin Xie, Senior Investment Strategist

### Page 5

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 23 October 2026

Top client questions (cont’d) 
  What has driven the recent weakness in China equities, 
and what is your outlook from here? 
Our view: We maintain China equities as a Core holding, as 
valuations remain inexpensive. Rapid technological innovation 
and investment in AI localisation provide tailwinds, but the 
potential for a re-rating appears delayed. Investors should seek 
to diversify their portfolios and reduce concentration risk. 
Rationale: China equities have weakened, driven by mounting 
scepticism over AI capex returns, structural margin 
compression and concerns about the country’s significant 
financing requirements. Another notable feature in China has been 
significant share issuance, which has diluted earnings per share and 
weighed on the MSCI China Index’s performance. These pressures 
have been compounded by geopolitical headwinds, restrictions on 
advanced chips, an enduring property sector contraction and weak 
domestic demand. September’s stimulus measures also 
disappointed markets in terms of their scale and pace of delivery. 
Notwithstanding these headwinds, valuations remain inexpensive, 
supported by rapid domestic innovation and structural tailwinds 
in AI localisation, leading us to retain China equities as a Core 
holding. Elevated volatility reinforces the need to be defensively 
positioned, favouring diversification rather than relying on technology 
sector concentration. We also remain Overweight China’s 
healthcare, financial and materials sectors. 
—  Cindy Lam, CFA, Senior Investment Strategist

Are you still bullish on US 10-year government bonds?   
Our view: We are closing our opportunistic bullish idea on US 
10-year government bonds at a gain of 1.7%. The risk-reward 
has become less compelling after the yield broke below 5%, the 
lower bound of our three-month forecast range of 5.00-5.25%. 
Rationale: The US 10-year yield fell over 17bps to 4.97% on 22 
October – its first move below 5% since 23 September and biggest 
one-day drop since March 2023. A sell-off in AI-related equities and 
renewed Middle East tensions drove investors towards safe havens. 
Earlier this month, the yield hit its highest level in over two decades. 
The forces behind the rally are fragile, though. Oil prices remain 
elevated amid escalating Middle East tensions, and September US 
consumer inflation rose to 3.7% y/y. Fed officials have signalled 
there is no urgency to raise rates at the 27-28 October meeting, 
but persistent price pressures keep further tightening on the table. 
We prefer to lock in gains and close our opportunistic bullish idea on 
10-year government bonds at a gain of 1.7%. 
—  Jonathan Liang, CFA, CIO, Fixed Income & FX

### Page 6

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 23 October 2026

Top client questions (cont’d) 
  What is your outlook for French bank bonds, especially 
ahead of France’s upcoming sovereign credit rating reviews?   
Our view: The recent sell-off in French bank bonds is a repricing 
of tail risks, not a credit story. We expect further price volatility 
until the April 2027 French presidential election, particularly at 
the junior end of the capital structure, so we are closing our 
opportunistic EU bank contingent convertibles (CoCos1) idea at 
a gain of 2.0%. 
Rationale: We believe the recent sell-off in French bank bonds 
reflects domestic political and fiscal risks rather than weaker 
credit fundamentals. The sustained widening of the OAT-Bund 
spread has raised concerns about the profit and loss (P&L) impact 
on banks' sovereign debt holdings. Banks provide limited 
disclosure on these exposures. However, banks’ investment 
portfolios are generally diversified, and holdings booked at amortised 
cost should soften the P&L impact. Near-term catalysts include the 
upcoming sovereign credit rating reviews. A sovereign downgrade 
could trigger bank rating downgrades, with domestically focused 
lenders potentially more exposed than regionally diversified peers. A 
sovereign credit event is not our base case, but we expect headline 
risks to persist until the French presidential election. Against this 
backdrop, we are closing our opportunistic EU bank CoCos idea. 
—  Cedric Lam, Senior Investment Strategist 
  Will the November US mid-term elections and European 
bond volatility support a sustained Swiss franc (CHF) rebound?  
Our view: We expect a near-term CHF rebound, supported 
mainly by safe-haven demand amid European bond volatility. 
We believe the US mid-term elections will be a secondary driver. 
Rationale: European sovereign bond volatility provides the stronger 
catalyst for CHF strength. The France-Germany government bond 
yield spread remains elevated near 160bps, reflecting heightened 
French fiscal concerns and reinforcing the CHF’s safe-haven appeal. 
The US mid-terms could provide additional support for the CHF. 
A Democratic victory in either chamber is expected to constrain 
further fiscal expansion, potentially weighing on the USD. However, 
this would not necessarily lead to faster fiscal consolidation. 
The SNB’s 0% policy rate leaves the CHF at a sizeable interest-
rate disadvantage vs. the USD, constraining sustained CHF gains. 
With EUR/CHF below 0.90, excessive CHF strength could also 
increase the risk of SNB intervention. A decisive USD/CHF break 
below 0.8130 would strengthen the technical case for a more 
sustained CHF rebound.  
—  Vincent Tan, Senior Investment Strategist 
—  Iris Yuen, Investment Strategist

A widening France-Germany 10-year government 
bond yield spread and a stable investment-grade 
(IG) bond option-adjusted spread (OAS) suggest a 
repricing of political and fiscal risk rather than 
credit risk

### Page 7

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 23 October 2026

Top client questions (cont’d) 
  Would worsening budget deficits and political uncertainty 
across parts of Europe affect your view on EUR/USD and Euro 
area government bond yields? 
Our view: Fiscal and political concerns constrain near-term 
EUR/USD gains, with France being the primary vulnerability. We 
expect EUR/USD to recover towards the 1.17-1.18 range over 
the next 12 months, supported by continued Euro area 
economic growth and an expected fading of USD support. We 
expect wider differentials in Euro area government bond yields, 
with political risk premium elevated in French government 
bonds (OATs) and German government bonds (Bunds) 
supported by safe-haven demand. 
Rationale: France's high public debt, large budget deficit and 
political obstacles to fiscal adjustments are weighing on market 
confidence, with the country’s 10-year government bond yield 
rising above 5%. The risk of stress spreading to Italy and Spain 
adds to regional concerns, although current evidence does not 
establish a broad European fiscal crisis. German government bonds 
should benefit from safe-haven demand, while fiscal concerns keep 
risk premia on vulnerable countries' bonds elevated. EUR/USD 
remains more sensitive to US-Germany interest-rate differentials 
and broader market confidence than to French bond yields alone. 
France’s fiscal risks, elevated energy costs and high US interest 
rates weigh on the EUR in the near term. Over the next 12 months, 
Germany’s multi-year public investment programme, resilient 
exports and sound banking-sector fundamentals are expected to 
support Euro area growth. We expect the Fed's rate-hiking cycle to 
peak by mid-2027, reducing USD support and driving the EUR  
towards 1.17-1.18 . 
The principal tail risk is fiscal stress widening government bond 
yield spreads and spilling over into bank funding conditions and 
lending activity, weakening growth and delaying the EUR's recovery. 
—  Vincent Tan, Senior Investment Strategist 
—  Cedric Lam, Senior Investment Strategist

Global Market Outlook 3

12m Foundation Overweights: 
• Gold

• Emerging Market USD bonds

• Global: High dividend 
• US: Healthcare^ 
• Euro area (EA): Banks, MSCI 
Switzerland^ 
• Asia: Japan banks

• US: Technology, healthcare^, 
financials, materials

• Asia: Japan financials

(CLOs), US High-yield (HY) 
• Others: AUD corporates  
^New 
Transitioning to mid-cycle 
• We now favour a more balanced stance between stocks and bonds. Near-
term event risks and high earnings expectations prompt us to trim equities to a 
Core holding. The US and Asia ex-Japan (AxJ) remain our preferred regions. 
Within AxJ, we upgrade India to Overweight. 
• Higher bond yields are improving the outlook for future returns and offering 
increasingly attractive income opportunities. We are Overweight Emerging 
Market (EM) USD bonds and favour corporate over government bonds, but limit 
duration to 3-7 years. 
• We remain Overweight gold. Prices are likely to grind higher as near-term 
headwinds from higher bond yields are offset by EM central bank demand. 
Great expectations 
Individual risk events, high earnings expectations and a higher cost of capital 
point to a more moderate return environment ahead. In the very near term, 
financial markets must navigate key event risks, such as (i) the US mid-term elections, 
which have historically been associated with elevated market volatility and (ii) renewed 
geopolitical tensions, with several reports suggesting the US is considering renewed 
strikes on Iran. These concerns have pushed the Brent oil price well above USD 
110/bbl despite significant ongoing crude oil flows through the Strait of Hormuz.  
Higher bond yields combined with high earnings expectations raise the risk of 
an expectations reset. The US Q3 earnings season is attracting significant attention 
given high earnings expectations, particularly for the AI sector. However, higher US 
bond yields may start to bite as debt-financed capex takes on a greater share of total 
capex over the coming year. This, combined with already-high expectations, raises the 
risk of disappointment and a near-term repricing of expected earnings growth pace.  
Despite the current challenges, healthy growth continues to underpin the 
economic backdrop. The US economic outlook remains well supported, and the 
absolute level of year-ahead earnings expectations is high. We do not expect Fed rate 
hikes to result in overly restrictive policy. Our caution, thus, stems from the risk of an 
expectations repricing rather than an outright economic slowdown.

1 Investment strategy and key themes

Steve Brice 
Global Chief Investment Officer 
Manpreet Gill 
Chief Investment Officer, EMEA 
Raymond Cheng, CFA, CPA 
Chief Investment Officer, North Asia

### Page 4

Global Market Outlook 4

Fig. 1 Our quant model score has fallen to levels 
associated with flat-to-modestly-negative equity returns  
 Fig. 2 Negative gold-USD correlation has held back 
gold amid USD strength. This is unlikely to persist 
Our quantitative stock-bond model  Gold and USD correlations over the last 15 years

Source: Bloomberg, Standard Chartered  Source: Bloomberg, Standard Chartered

A more balanced stock-bond mix 
We trim global equities to a Core holding, leaving us 
Neutral on both equities and bonds and Overweight gold. 
The concerns laid out earlier point to near-term volatility risks 
and a somewhat lower long-term growth trajectory for 
equities. Our quant model also reflects this cautious outlook, 
having downgraded equities to a level usually associated with 
flat-to-modestly-negative returns. 
From a relative perspective, though, the higher yields on 
offer from bonds also raise the bar by presenting an 
increasingly compelling return outlook after several 
years of very low yields. We believe this argues for portfolios 
that are increasingly balanced between equities and bonds. 
Ways to diversify equity exposure 
We continue to favour global equity market 
diversification, with a preference for US and AxJ equities. 
We remain Overweight US and AxJ equities, expecting them 
to outperform other major regions. Earnings growth remains 
the main driver of this view – while growth rates could 
decelerate somewhat, we still expect them to remain higher 
than those of other major regions.  
Japan and Euro area equities remain attractive as 
regional diversifiers. Our positive view on both markets’ 
financial sectors supports our Core holding view on them.   
Within Asia, we are Overweight Taiwan and India. Indian 
equities have lagged the region this year, in part due to limited 
listed AI-related exposure. However, we believe this is 
increasingly likely to become an advantage as investors seek 
greater diversification away from excessive concentration in 
the AI theme. This comes at a time when the economic and 
earnings growth outlook for Indian equities is starting to 
improve. We retain a more Neutral view on Chinese equities 
amid a still-challenging growth outlook. 
We also favour greater sector diversification. In the US, 
we favour diversifying across our Overweight sectors of 
technology, financials, materials and healthcare. We also 
favour financials in Japan and the Euro area.  
Changes to our equity opportunistic ideas also reflect a 
more defensive stance. In recent weeks, we have closed our 
global semiconductors, MSCI Taiwan, US communication 
services and global power & electrification ideas and have 
opened new ideas on US healthcare and MSCI Switzerland. 
Riding the yield train 
We believe bond markets offer an attractive opportunity 
to lock in yields. The 10-year US government bond yield is 
now in a range last witnessed in 2007. While the process of 
rising yields results in lower bond prices – as painfully 
experienced in recent months – the yield level itself starts to 
significantly improve the outlook for future returns. 
Favour corporate over government bonds, but check 
your duration. Still-high credit quality means that we continue 
to favour taking on credit risk to earn a higher yield (EM USD 
bonds are preferred, but we also see investment-grade (IG) 
and high-yield (HY) bonds as Core holdings). However, 
limiting duration to 3-7 years remains key to avoiding 
excessive exposure to any inflation surprises. 
Changes to our bond opportunistic ideas also reflect a 
more balanced stance. We close our EU bank contingent 
convertible bonds (CoCos2 – please refer page 29) and US 
utilities corporate hybrids ideas. We also close our 
opportunistic idea on US 10-year government bonds following 
the recent pullback in yields, locking in gains. 
Alternatives and gold to remain relevant 
Alternatives are an effective tool for portfolio risk 
management. Hedge funds and private markets provide a 
differentiated return stream, while rotating into alternatives 
helps reduce equity beta exposure. We examine downside 
capture and how beta is reduced via hedge fund sub-
strategies and private markets in our asset allocation section.  
Gold remains a key Overweight. We trim our three- and 12-
month forecasts modestly, but see gold forming a strong base 
above USD 4,000/oz. Rising bond yields and the USD were 
recent headwinds, but we expect a softer USD and continued 
EM central bank demand to result in 12-month gains.
